### p1
1. Gaṇapatyatharvaśīrṣopaniṣat

om̐ bha̱draṃ karṇe̍bhiḥ śṛṇu̱yāma̍ devāḥ |

bha̱draṃ pa̍śyemā̱kṣabhi̱ryaja̍trāḥ |

sthi̱rairaṅgai̎stuṣṭu̱vāgṃ sa̍sta̱nūbhi̍ḥ |

vyaśe̍ma de̱vahi̍ta̱ṃ yadāyu̍ḥ |

sva̱sti na̱ indro̍ vṛ̱ddhaśra̍vāḥ |

sva̱sti na̍ḥ pū̱ṣā vi̱śvave̍dāḥ |

sva̱sti na̱stārkṣyo̱ ari̍ṣṭanemiḥ |

sva̱sti no̱ bṛha̱spati̍rdadhātu ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

om̐ nama̍ste ga̱ṇapa̍taye |

tvame̱va pra̱tyakṣa̱ṃ tattva̍masi |

tvame̱va ke̱vala̱ṃ kartā̍'si |

tvame̱va ke̱vala̱ṃ dhartā̍'si |

tvame̱va ke̱vala̱ṃ hartā̍'si |

tvameva sarvaṃ khalvida̍ṃ brahmā̱si |

tvaṃ sākṣādātmā̍'si ni̱tyam || 1 ||

ṛ̍taṃ va̱cmi | sa̍tyaṃ va̱cmi || 2 ||

ava̱ tva̱ṃ mām | ava̍ va̱ktāra̎m |

ava̍ śro̱tāra̎m | ava̍ dā̱tāra̎m |

ava̍ dhā̱tāra̎m | avānūcānama̍va śi̱ṣyam |

ava̍ pa̱ścāttā̎t | ava̍ pu̱rastā̎t |

avotta̱rāttā̎t | ava̍ dakṣi̱ṇāttā̎t |

ava̍ co̱rdhvāttā̎t | avādha̱rāttā̎t |

sarvato māṃ pāhi pāhi̍ sama̱ntāt || 3 ||

tvaṃ vāṅmaya̍stvaṃ cinma̱yaḥ |

tvamānandamaya̍stvaṃ brahma̱mayaḥ |

tvaṃ saccidānandā'dvi̍tīyo̱'si |

tvaṃ pra̱tyakṣa̱ṃ brahmā̍si |

tvaṃ jñānamayo vijñāna̍mayo̱'si || 4 ||

sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ tva̍tto jā̱yate |

sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ tva̍ttasti̱ṣṭhati |

sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ tvayi laya̍meṣya̱ti |

sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ tvayi̍ pratye̱ti |

tvaṃ bhūmirāpo'nalo'ni̍lo na̱bhaḥ |

tvaṃ catvāri vā̎kpadā̱ni || 5 ||

tvaṃ gu̱ṇatra̍yātī̱taḥ |

tvaṃ avasthātra̍yātī̱taḥ |

tvaṃ de̱hatra̍yātī̱taḥ |

tvaṃ kā̱latra̍yātī̱taḥ |

tvaṃ mūlādhārasthito̍'si ni̱tyam |

tvaṃ śaktitra̍yātma̱kaḥ |

tvāṃ yogino dhyāya̍nti ni̱tyam |

tvaṃ brahmā tvaṃ viṣṇustvaṃ rudrastvamindrastvamagnistvaṃ

vāyustvaṃ sūryastvaṃ candramāstvaṃ brahma̱ bhūrbhuva̱ḥ svarom || 6 ||

ga̱ṇādi̎ṃ pūrva̍muccā̱rya̱ va̱rṇādī̎ṃ stadana̱ntaram |

anusvāraḥ pa̍rata̱raḥ | ardhe̎ndula̱sitam |

tāre̍ṇa ṛ̱ddham | etattava manu̍svarū̱pam |

gakāraḥ pū̎rvarū̱pam | akāro madhya̍marū̱pam |

anusvāraścā̎ntyarū̱pam | bindurutta̍rarū̱pam |

nāda̍ḥ sandhā̱nam | sagṃhi̍tā sa̱ndhiḥ |

saiṣā gaṇe̍śavi̱dyā | gaṇa̍ka ṛ̱ṣiḥ |

nicṛdgāya̍trīccha̱ndaḥ | gaṇapati̍rdeva̱tā |

om̐ gaṃ ga̱ṇapa̍taye namaḥ || 7 ||

ekada̱ntāya̍ vi̱dmahe̍ vakratu̱ṇḍāya̍ dhīmahi |

tanno̍ dantiḥ praco̱dayā̎t || 8 ||

ekada̱ntaṃ ca̍turha̱sta̱ṃ pā̱śama̍ṅkuśa̱dhāri̍ṇam |

rada̍ṃ ca̱ vara̍daṃ ha̱stai̱rbi̱bhrāṇa̍ṃ mūṣa̱kadhva̍jam |

rakta̍ṃ la̱mboda̍raṃ śū̱rpa̱ka̱rṇaka̍ṃ rakta̱vāsa̍sam |

rakta̍ga̱ndhānu̍liptā̱ṅga̱ṃ ra̱ktapu̍ṣpaiḥ su̱pūji̍tam |

bhaktā̍nu̱kampi̍naṃ de̱va̱ṃ ja̱gatkā̍raṇa̱macyu̍tam |

āvi̍rbhū̱taṃ ca̍ sṛ̱ṣṭyā̱dau̱ pra̱kṛte̎ḥ puru̱ṣātpa̍ram |

eva̍ṃ dhyā̱yati̍ yo ni̱tya̱ṃ sa̱ yogī̍ yogi̱nāṃ va̍raḥ || 9 ||

namo vrātapataye namo gaṇapataye

namaḥ pramathapataye

namaste'stu lambodarāyaikadantāya

vighnanāśine śivasutāya

varadamūrtaye̱ nama̍ḥ || 10 ||

etadatharvaśīrṣa̍ṃ yo'dhī̱te sa brahmabhūyā̍ya ka̱lpate |

sa sarvavighnai̎rna bā̱dhyate | sa sarvatra sukha̍medha̱te |

sa pañcamahāpāpā̎t pramu̱cyate |

sā̱yama̍dhīyā̱no̱ divasakṛtaṃ pāpa̍ṃ nāśa̱yati |

prā̱tara̍dhīyā̱no̱ rātri̍kṛtaṃ pāpa̍ṃ nāśa̱yati |

sāyaṃ prātaḥ pra̍yuñjā̱no̱ pāpo'pā̍po bha̱vati |

sarvatrādhīyāno'pavi̍ghno bha̱vati |

dharmārthakāmamokṣa̍ṃ ca vi̱ndati |

idamatharvaśīrṣamaśiṣyāya̍ na de̱yam |

yo yadi mo̍hād dā̱syati sa pāpī̍yān bha̱vati |

sahasrāvartanādyaṃ yaṃ kāma̍madhī̱te taṃ tamane̍na sā̱dhayet || 11 ||

anena gaṇapatima̍bhiṣi̱ñcati sa vā̍gmī bha̱vati |

caturthyāmana̍śnan ja̱pati sa vidyā̍vān bha̱vati |

ityatharva̍ṇavā̱kyam |

brahmādyā̱vara̍ṇaṃ vi̱dyānna bibheti kadā̍cane̱ti || 12 ||

yo dūrvāṅku̍rairya̱jati sa vaiśravaṇopa̍mo bha̱vati |

yo lā̍jairya̱jati sa yaśo̍vān bha̱vati |

sa medhā̍vān bha̱vati |

yo modakasahasre̍ṇa ya̱jati sa vāñchitaphalama̍vāpno̱ti |

yaḥ sājya sami̍dbhirya̱jati

sa sarvaṃ labhate sa sa̍rvaṃ la̱bhate || 13 ||

aṣṭau brāhmaṇān samyag grā̍hayi̱tvā sūryavarca̍svī bha̱vati |

sūryagrahe ma̍hāna̱dyāṃ pratimāsannidhau vā ja̱ptvā siddhama̍ntro bha̱vati |

mahāvighnā̎t pramu̱cyate | mahādoṣā̎t pramu̱cyate |

mahāpratyavāyā̎t pramu̱cyate |

sa sarvavidbhavati sa sarva̍vidbha̱vati |

ya e̍vaṃ ve̱da | ityu̍pa̱niṣa̍t || 14 ||

om̐ bha̱draṃ karṇe̍bhiḥ śṛṇu̱yāma̍ devāḥ |

bha̱draṃ pa̍śyemā̱kṣabhi̱ryaja̍trāḥ |

sthi̱rairaṅgai̎stuṣṭu̱vāgṃ sa̍sta̱nūbhi̍ḥ |

vyaśe̍ma de̱vahi̍ta̱ṃ yadāyu̍ḥ |

sva̱sti na̱ indro̍ vṛ̱ddhaśra̍vāḥ |

sva̱sti na̍ḥ pū̱ṣā vi̱śvave̍dāḥ |

sva̱sti na̱stārkṣyo̱ ari̍ṣṭanemiḥ |

sva̱sti no̱ bṛha̱spati̍rdadhātu ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

An Atharva Veda Upanishad ↗

### vedic-chant-purusha-suktam
2. Puruṣasūktam

om̐ taccha̱ṃ yorāvṛ̍ṇīmahe |

gā̱tuṃ ya̱jñāya̍ | gā̱tuṃ ya̱jñapa̍taye |

daivī̎ḥ sva̱stira̍stu naḥ |

sva̱stirmānu̍ṣebhyaḥ |

ū̱rdhvaṃ ji̍gātu bheṣa̱jam |

śanno̍ astu dvi̱pade̎ | śaṃ catu̍ṣpade ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

om̐ sa̱hasra̍śīrṣā̱ puru̍ṣaḥ |

sa̱ha̱srā̱kṣaḥ sa̱hasra̍pāt |

sa bhūmi̍ṃ vi̱śvato̍ vṛ̱tvā |

atya̍tiṣṭhaddaśāṅgu̱lam |

puru̍ṣa e̱vedagṃ sarvam̎ |

yadbhū̱taṃ yacca̱ bhavyam̎ |

u̱tāmṛ̍ta̱tvasyeśā̍naḥ |

ya̱danne̍nāti̱roha̍ti |

e̱tāvā̍nasya mahi̱mā |

ato̱ jyāyāg̍śca̱ pūru̍ṣaḥ || 1 ||

pādo̎'sya̱ viśvā̍ bhū̱tāni̍ |

tri̱pāda̍syā̱mṛta̍ṃ di̱vi |

tri̱pādū̱rdhva udai̱tpuru̍ṣaḥ |

pādo̎'sye̱hā''bha̍vā̱tpuna̍ḥ |

tato̱ viṣva̱ṅvya̍krāmat |

sā̱śa̱nā̱na̱śa̱ne a̱bhi |

tasmā̎dvi̱rāḍa̍jāyata |

vi̱rājo̱ adhi̱ pūru̍ṣaḥ |

sa jā̱to atya̍ricyata |

pa̱ścādbhūmi̱matho̍ pu̱raḥ || 2 ||

yatpuru̍ṣeṇa ha̱viṣā̎ |

de̱vā ya̱jñamata̍nvata |

va̱sa̱nto a̍syāsī̱dājyam̎ |

grī̱ṣma i̱dhmaśśa̱raddha̱viḥ |

sa̱ptāsyā̍sanpari̱dhaya̍ḥ |

triḥ sa̱pta sa̱midha̍ḥ kṛ̱tāḥ |

de̱vā yadya̱jñaṃ ta̍nvā̱nāḥ |

aba̍dhna̱npuru̍ṣaṃ pa̱śum |

taṃ ya̱jñaṃ ba̱ṟhiṣi̱ praukṣan̍ |

puru̍ṣaṃ jā̱tama̍gra̱taḥ || 3 ||

tena̍ de̱vā aya̍janta |

sā̱dhyā ṛṣa̍yaśca̱ ye |

tasmā̎dya̱jñātsa̍rva̱huta̍ḥ |

sambhṛ̍taṃ pṛṣadā̱jyam |

pa̱śūgstāgśca̍kre vāya̱vyān̍ |

ā̱ra̱ṇyāngrā̱myāśca̱ ye |

tasmā̎dya̱jñātsa̍rva̱huta̍ḥ |

ṛca̱ḥ sāmā̍ni jajñire |

chandāgṃ̍si jajñire̱ tasmā̎t |

yaju̱stasmā̍dajāyata || 4 ||

tasmā̱daśvā̍ ajāyanta |

ye ke co̍bha̱yāda̍taḥ |

gāvo̍ ha jajñire̱ tasmā̎t |

tasmā̎jjā̱tā a̍jā̱vaya̍ḥ |

yatpuru̍ṣa̱ṃ vya̍dadhuḥ |

ka̱ti̱dhā vya̍kalpayan |

mukha̱ṃ kima̍sya̱ kau bā̱hū |

kāvū̱rū pādā̍vucyete |

brā̱hma̱ṇo̎'sya̱ mukha̍māsīt |

bā̱hū rā̍ja̱nya̍ḥ kṛ̱taḥ || 5 ||

ū̱rū tada̍sya̱ yadvaiśya̍ḥ |

pa̱dbhyāgṃ śū̱dro a̍jāyata |

ca̱ndramā̱ mana̍so jā̱taḥ |

cakṣo̱ḥ sūryo̍ ajāyata |

mukhā̱dindra̍ścā̱gniśca̍ |

prā̱ṇādvā̱yura̍jāyata |

nābhyā̍ āsīda̱ntari̍kṣam |

śī̱rṣṇo dyauḥ sama̍vartata |

pa̱dbhyāṃ bhūmi̱rdiśa̱ḥ śrotrā̎t |

tathā̍ lo̱kāgṃ a̍kalpayan || 6 ||

vedā̱hame̱taṃ puru̍ṣaṃ ma̱hāntam̎ |

ā̱di̱tyava̍rṇa̱ṃ tama̍sa̱stu pā̱re |

sarvā̍ṇi rū̱pāṇi̍ vi̱citya̱ dhīra̍ḥ |

nāmā̍ni kṛ̱tvā'bhi̱vada̱ṉ, yadāste̎ |

dhā̱tā pu̱rastā̱dyamu̍dāja̱hāra̍ |

śa̱kraḥ pravi̱dvānpra̱diśa̱ścata̍sraḥ |

tame̱vaṃ vi̱dvāna̱mṛta̍ i̱ha bha̍vati |

nānyaḥ panthā̱ aya̍nāya vidyate |

ya̱jñena̍ ya̱jñama̍yajanta de̱vāḥ |

tāni̱ dharmā̍ṇi pratha̱mānyā̍san |

te ha̱ nāka̍ṃ mahi̱māna̍ḥ sacante |

yatra̱ pūrve̍ sā̱dhyāḥ santi̍ de̱vāḥ || 7 ||

a̱dbhyaḥ sambhū̍taḥ pṛthi̱vyai rasā̎cca |

vi̱śvaka̍rmaṇa̱ḥ sama̍varta̱tādhi̍ |

tasya̱ tvaṣṭā̍ vi̱dadha̍drū̱pame̍ti |

tatpuru̍ṣasya̱ viśva̱mājā̍na̱magre̎ |

vedā̱hame̱taṃ puru̍ṣaṃ ma̱hāntam̎ |

ā̱di̱tyava̍rṇa̱ṃ tama̍sa̱ḥ para̍stāt |

tame̱vaṃ vi̱dvāna̱mṛta̍ i̱ha bha̍vati |

nānyaḥ panthā̍ vidya̱te'ya̍nāya |

pra̱jāpa̍tiścarati̱ garbhe̍ a̱ntaḥ |

a̱jāya̍māno bahu̱dhā vijā̍yate || 8 ||

tasya̱ dhīrā̱ḥ pari̍jānanti̱ yonim̎ |

marī̍cīnāṃ pa̱dami̍cchanti ve̱dhasa̍ḥ |

yo de̱vebhya̱ āta̍pati |

yo de̱vānā̎ṃ pu̱rohi̍taḥ |

pūrvo̱ yo de̱vebhyo̍ jā̱taḥ |

namo̍ ru̱cāya̱ brāhma̍ye |

ruca̍ṃ brā̱hmaṃ ja̱naya̍ntaḥ |

de̱vā agre̱ tada̍bruvan |

yastvai̱vaṃ brā̎hma̱ṇo vi̱dyāt |

tasya̍ de̱vā asa̱n vaśe̎ || 9 ||

hrīśca̍ te la̱kṣmīśca̱ patnyau̎ |

a̱ho̱rā̱tre pā̱rśve |

nakṣa̍trāṇi rū̱pam |

a̱śvinau̱ vyāttam̎ |

i̱ṣṭaṃ ma̍niṣāṇa | a̱muṃ ma̍niṣāṇa |

sarva̍ṃ maniṣāṇa || 10 ||

om̐ taccha̱ṃ yorāvṛ̍ṇīmahe |

gā̱tuṃ ya̱jñāya̍ | gā̱tuṃ ya̱jñapa̍taye |

daivī̎ḥ sva̱stira̍stu naḥ |

sva̱stirmānu̍ṣebhyaḥ |

ū̱rdhvaṃ ji̍gātu bheṣa̱jam |

śanno̍ astu dvi̱pade̎ | śaṃ catu̍ṣpade ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

Krishna Yajurveda, Taittiriya Aranyaka ↗

### vedic-chant-shri-sukta
3. Śrīsūkta

om̐ || hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīṃ suvarṇarajatasrajām |

candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 1||

tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm |

yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ vindeyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣānaham || 2||

aśvapūrvāṃ rathamadhyāṃ hastinādaprabodhinīm |

śriyaṃ devīmupahvaye śrīrmādevīrjuṣatām || 3||

kāṃ sosmitāṃ hiraṇyaprākārāmārdrāṃ jvalantīṃ tṛptāṃ tarpayantīm |

padme sthitāṃ padmavarṇāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam || 4||

candrāṃ prabhāsāṃ yaśasā jvalantīṃ śriyaṃ loke devajuṣṭāmudārām |

tāṃ padminīmīṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye'lakṣmīrme naśyatāṃ tvāṃ vṛṇe || 5||

ādityavarṇe tapaso'dhijāto vanaspatistava vṛkṣo'tha bilvaḥ |

tasya phalāni tapasā nudantu māyāntarāyāśca bāhyā alakṣmīḥ || 6||

upaitu māṃ devasakhaḥ kīrtiśca maṇinā saha |

prādurbhūto'smi rāṣṭre'smin kīrtimṛddhiṃ dadātu me || 7||

kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ jyeṣṭhāmalakṣmīṃ nāśayāmyaham |

abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca sarvāṃ nirṇuda me gṛhāt || 8||

gandhadvārāṃ durādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭāṃ karīṣiṇīm |

īśvarīgṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tāmihopahvaye śriyam || 9||

manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ vācaḥ satyamaśīmahi |

paśūnāṃ rūpamannasya mayi śrīḥ śrayatāṃ yaśaḥ || 10||

kardamena prajābhūtā mayi sambhava kardama |

śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule mātaraṃ padmamālinīm || 11||

āpaḥ sṛjantu snigdhāni ciklīta vasa me gṛhe |

ni ca devīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule || 12||

ārdrāṃ puṣkariṇīṃ puṣṭiṃ piṅgalāṃ padmamālinīm |

candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 13||

ārdrāṃ yaḥ kariṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ suvarṇāṃ hemamālinīm |

sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo ma āvaha || 14||

tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm |

yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ prabhūtaṃ gāvo dāsyo'śvānvindeyaṃ puruṣānaham || 15||

yaḥ śuciḥ prayato bhūtvā juhuyādājya manvaham |

śriyaḥ pañcadaśarcaṃ ca śrīkāmaḥ satataṃ japet || 16||

Phalashruti

padmānane padma ūrū padmākṣī padmasambhave |

tvaṃ māṃ bhajasva padmākṣī yena saukhyaṃ labhāmyaham ||

aśvadāyī godāyī dhanadāyī mahādhane |

dhanaṃ me juṣatāṃ devi sarvakāmāṃśca dehi me ||

putrapautra dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ hastyaśvādigave ratham |

prajānāṃ bhavasi mātā āyuṣmantaṃ karotu mām ||

dhanamagnirdhanaṃ vāyurdhanaṃ sūryo dhanaṃ vasuḥ |

dhanamindro bṛhaspatirvaruṇaṃ dhanamaśnu te ||

vainateya somaṃ piba somaṃ pibatu vṛtrahā |

somaṃ dhanasya somino mahyaṃ dadātu sominaḥ ||

na krodho na ca mātsaryaṃ na lobho nāśubhā matiḥ ||

bhavanti kṛtapuṇyānāṃ bhaktānāṃ śrīsūktaṃ japetsadā ||

varṣantu te vibhāvari divo abhrasya vidyutaḥ |

rohantu sarvabījānyava brahma dviṣo jahi ||

padmapriye padmini padmahaste padmālaye padmadalāyatākṣi |

viśvapriye viṣṇu mano'nukūle tvatpādapadmaṃ mayi sannidhatsva ||

yā sā padmāsanasthā vipulakaṭitaṭī padmapatrāyatākṣī |

gambhīrā vartanābhiḥ stanabhara namitā śubhra vastrottarīyā |

lakṣmīrdivyairgajendrairmaṇigaṇa khacitaissnāpitā hemakumbhaiḥ |

nityaṃ sā padmahastā mama vasatu gṛhe sarvamāṅgalyayuktā ||

lakṣmīṃ kṣīrasamudra rājatanayāṃ śrīraṅgadhāmeśvarīm |

dāsībhūtasamasta deva vanitāṃ lokaika dīpāṅkurām |

śrīmanmandakaṭākṣalabdha vibhava brahmendragaṅgādharām |

tvāṃ trailokya kuṭumbinīṃ sarasijāṃ vande mukundapriyām ||

siddhalakṣmīrmokṣalakṣmīrjayalakṣmīssarasvatī |

śrīlakṣmīrvaralakṣmīśca prasannā mama sarvadā ||

varāṅkuśau pāśamabhītimudrāṃ karairvahantīṃ kamalāsanasthām |

bālārka koṭi pratibhāṃ triṇetrāṃ bhajehamādyāṃ jagadīśvarīṃ tām ||

sarvamaṅgalamāṅgalye śive sarvārtha sādhike |

śaraṇye tryambake devi nārāyaṇi namo'stu te ||

sarasijanilaye sarojahaste dhavalatarāṃśuka gandhamālyaśobhe |

bhagavati harivallabhe manojñe tribhuvanabhūtikariprasīda mahyam ||

viṣṇupatnīṃ kṣamāṃ devīṃ mādhavīṃ mādhavapriyām |

viṣṇoḥ priyasakhīmm devīṃ namāmyacyutavallabhām ||

mahālakṣmī ca vidmahe viṣṇupatnī ca dhīmahī |

tanno lakṣmīḥ pracodayāt ||

Variant reading

ānandaḥ kardamaḥ śrīdaściklīta iti viśrutāḥ |

ṛṣayaḥ śriyaḥ putrāśca śrīrdevīrdevatā matāḥ (svayam

śrīreva devatā || )

Variant reading

candrabhāṃ lakṣmīmīśānām sūryabhāṃ śriyamīśvarīm |

candra sūryagni sarvābhām śrīmahālakṣmīmupāsmahe ||

śrīvarcasyamāyuṣyamārogyamāvidhāt pavamānaṃ mahīyate |

dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ paśuṃ bahuputralābhaṃ śatasaṃvatsaraṃ dīrghamāyuḥ ||

ṛṇarogādidāridryapāpakṣudapamṛtyavaḥ |

bhayaśokamanastāpā naśyantu mama sarvadā ||

śriye jāta śriya āniryāya śriyaṃ vayo janitṛbhyo dadhātu |

śriyaṃ vasānā amṛtatvamāyan bhajanti sadyaḥ savitā vidadhyūn ||

śriya evainaṃ tacchriyāmādadhāti | santatamṛcā vaṣaṭkṛtyaṃ

sandhattaṃ sandhīyate prajayā paśubhiḥ | ya evaṃ veda |

om̐ mahādevyai ca vidmahe viṣṇupatnī ca dhīmahi |

tanno lakṣmīḥ pracodayāt ||

|| om̐ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||

Rig Veda (Khila Sukta)

### vedic-chant-laghunyasa
4. Laghunyāsaḥ

om̐ athātmānagṃ śivātmānag śrīrudrarūpaṃ dhyāyet ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ trinetraṃ pañcavaktrakam |

gaṅgādharaṃ daśabhujaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

nīlagrīvaṃ śaśāṅkāṅkaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |

vyāghracarmottarīyaṃ ca vareṇyamabhayapradam ||

kamaṇḍalvakṣasūtrāṇāṃ dhāriṇaṃ śūlapāṇinam |

jvalantaṃ piṅgalajaṭā-śikhāmudyotadhāriṇam ||

vṛṣaskandha-samārūḍham umādehārdha-dhāriṇam |

amṛtenāplutaṃ śāntaṃ divyabhogasamanvitam ||

digdevatāsamāyuktaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam |

nityaṃ ca śāśvataṃ śuddhaṃ dhruvamakṣaramavyayam ||

sarvavyāpinamīśānaṃ rudraṃ vai viśvarūpiṇam |

evaṃ dhyātvā dvijasamyak tato yajanamārabhet ||

om̐ prajanane brahmā tiṣṭhatu | pādayorviṣṇustiṣṭhatu |

hastayorharastiṣṭhatu | bāhvorindrastiṣṭhatu |

jaṭhare'gnistiṣṭhatu | hṛdaye śivastiṣṭhatu |

kaṇṭhe vasavastiṣṭhantu | vaktre sarasvatī tiṣṭhatu |

nāsikayorvāyustiṣṭhatu | nayanayoścandrādityau tiṣṭhetām |

karṇayoraśvinau tiṣṭhetām | lalāṭe rudrāstiṣṭhantu |

mūrdhnyādityāstiṣṭhantu | śirasi mahādevastiṣṭhatu |

śikhāyāṃ vāmadevastiṣṭhatu | pṛṣṭhe pinākī tiṣṭhatu |

purataḥ śūlī tiṣṭhatu | pārśvayoḥ śivāśaṅkarau tiṣṭhetām |

sarvato vāyustiṣṭhatu | tato bahiḥ sarvato'gnirjvālāmālā parivṛtastiṣṭhatu |

sarveṣvaṅgeṣu sarvā devatā yathāsthānaṃ tiṣṭhantu |

māgṃ rakṣantu | sarvān mahājanān rakṣantu ||

a̱gnirme̍ vā̱ci śri̱taḥ | vāgdhṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

vā̱yurme̎ prā̱ṇe śri̱taḥ | prā̱ṇo hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

sūryo̍ me̱ cakṣuṣi śri̱taḥ | cakṣu̱rhṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

ca̱ndramā̍ me̱ mana̍si śri̱taḥ | mano̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

diśo̍ me̱ śrotre̎ śri̱tāḥ | śrotra̱gṃ̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

āpo̍ me̱ reta̍si śri̱tāḥ | reto̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

pṛ̱thi̱vī me̱ śarī̍re śri̱tā | śarī̍ra̱gṃ̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

o̱ṣa̱dhi̱va̱na̱spa̱tayo̍ me̱ loma̍su śri̱tāḥ | lomā̍ni̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

indro̍ me̱ bale̎ śri̱taḥ | bala̱gṃ̱ hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

pa̱rjanyo̍ me mū̱rdhni śri̱taḥ | mū̱rdhā hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

īśā̍no me ma̱nyau śri̱taḥ | ma̱nyurhṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

ā̱tmā ma̍ ā̱tmani̍ śri̱taḥ | ā̱tmā hṛda̍ye |

hṛda̍ya̱ṃ mayi̍ | a̱hama̱mṛte̎ | a̱mṛta̱ṃ brahma̍ṇi |

puna̍rma ā̱tmā puna̱rāyu̱rāgā̎t |

puna̍ḥ prā̱ṇaḥ puna̱rākū̍ta̱māgā̎t |

vai̱śvā̱na̱ro ra̱śmibhi̍rvāvṛdhā̱naḥ |

a̱ntasti̍ṣṭhatva̱mṛta̍sya go̱pāḥ ||

asya śrī rudrādhyāya praśna mahāmantrasya aghora ṛṣiḥ, anuṣṭup chandaḥ,

saṅkarṣaṇamūrtisvarūpo yo'sāvādityaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ sa eṣa rudro devatā |

namaḥ śivāyeti bījam | śivatarāyeti śaktiḥ | mahādevāyeti kīlakam |

śrī sāmbasadāśiva prasāda siddhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ||

om̐ agnihotrātmane aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsātmane tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ |

cāturmāsyātmane madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | nirūḍhapaśubandhātmane anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ |

jyotiṣṭomātmane kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ | sarvakratvātmane karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |

agnihotrātmane hṛdayāya namaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsātmane śirase svāhā |

cāturmāsyātmane śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | nirūḍhapaśubandhātmane kavacāya hum |

jyotiṣṭomātmane netratrayāya vauṣaṭ | sarvakratvātmane astrāya phaṭ |

bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbandhaḥ |

Dhyanam

āpātāḷa-nabhaḥ sthalānta-bhuvana-brahmāṇḍa-māvisphura-

jjyotiḥ sphāṭika-liṅga-mauḷi-vilasatpūrṇendu-vāntāmṛtaiḥ |

astokāpluta-meka-mīśa-maniśaṃ rudrānuvākāñjapan

dhyāye-dīpsita-siddhaye dhruvapadaṃ vipro'bhiṣiñce-cchivam ||

brahmāṇḍavyāptadehā bhasitahimarucā bhāsamānā bhujaṅgaiḥ

kaṇṭhe kālāḥ kapardākalita śaśikalā-ścaṇḍakodaṇḍa hastāḥ |

tryakṣā rudrākṣamālāḥ prakaṭitavibhavāḥ śāmbhavā mūrtibhedā

rudrāḥ śrīrudrasūkta-prakaṭitavibhavā naḥ prayacchantu saukhyam ||

om̐ ga̱ṇānā̎ṃ tvā ga̱ṇapa̍tigṃ havāmahe ka̱viṃ ka̍vī̱nāmu̍pa̱maśra̍vastamam |

jye̱ṣṭha̱rāja̱ṃ brahma̍ṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata̱ ā na̍ḥ śṛ̱ṇvannū̱tibhi̍ssīda̱ sāda̍nam ||

mahāgaṇapataye̱ namaḥ ||

śaṃ ca̍ me̱ maya̍śca me pri̱yaṃ ca̍ me'nukā̱maśca̍ me̱ kāma̍śca me

saumana̱saśca̍ me bha̱draṃ ca̍ me̱ śreya̍śca me̱ vasya̍śca me̱ yaśa̍śca me̱

bhaga̍śca me̱ dravi̍ṇaṃ ca me ya̱ntā ca me dha̱rtā ca̍ me̱ kṣema̍śca me̱

dhṛti̍śca me̱ viśva̍ṃ ca me̱ maha̍śca me sa̱ṃvicca̍ me̱ jñātra̍ṃ ca me̱

sūśca̍ me pra̱sūśca̍ me̱ sīra̍ṃ ca me la̱yaśca̍ ma ṛ̱taṃ ca̍ me̱'mṛta̍ṃ ca

me'ya̱kṣmaṃ ca̱ me'nā̍mayacca me jī̱vātuśca me dīrghāyu̱tvaṃ ca̍ me'nami̱traṃ ca̱

me'bha̍yaṃ ca me su̱gaṃ ca̍ me̱ śaya̍naṃ ca me sū̱ṣā ca̍ me su̱dina̍ṃ ca me ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

### vedic-chant-shree-rudra
5. Śrī Rudrapraśnaḥ

om̐ namo bhagavate̍ rudrā̱ya ||

om̐ nama̍ste rudra ma̱nyava̍ u̱tota̱ iṣa̍ve̱ nama̍ḥ |

nama̍ste astu̱ dhanva̍ne bā̱hubhyā̍mu̱ta te̱ nama̍ḥ |

yā ta̱ iṣu̍ḥ śi̱vata̍mā śi̱vaṃ ba̱bhūva̍ te̱ dhanu̍ḥ |

śi̱vā śa̍ra̱vyā̍ yā tava̱ tayā̍ no rudra mṛḍaya |

yā te̍ rudra śi̱vā ta̱nūragho̱rā'pā̍pakāśinī |

tayā̍ nasta̱nuvā̱ śanta̍mayā̱ giri̍śantā̱bhicā̍kaśīhi |

yāmiṣu̍ṃ giriśanta̱ haste̱ bibha̱rṣyasta̍ve |

śi̱vāṃ gi̍ritra̱ tāṃ ku̍ru̱ mā higṃ̍sī̱ḥ puru̍ṣa̱ṃ jaga̍t |

śi̱vena̱ vaca̍sā tvā̱ giri̱śācchā̍vadāmasi |

yathā̍ na̱ḥ sarva̱mijjaga̍daya̱kṣmagṃ su̱manā̱ asa̍t |

adhya̍vocadadhiva̱ktā pra̍tha̱mo daivyo̍ bhi̱ṣak |

ahīg̍śca̱ sarvā̎ñja̱mbhaya̱ntsarvā̎śca yātudhā̱nya̍ḥ |

a̱sau yastā̱mro a̍ru̱ṇa u̱ta ba̱bhruḥ su̍ma̱ṅgala̍ḥ |

ye ce̱māgṃ ru̱drā a̱bhito̍ di̱kṣu śri̱tāḥ

sa̍hasra̱śo'vai̍ṣā̱gṃ̱ heḍa̍ īmahe |

a̱sau yo̍'va̱sarpa̍ti̱ nīla̍grīvo̱ vilo̍hitaḥ |

u̱taina̍ṃ go̱pā a̍dṛśa̱nnadṛ̍śannudahā̱rya̍ḥ |

u̱taina̱ṃ viśvā̍ bhū̱tāni̱ sa dṛ̱ṣṭo mṛ̍ḍayāti naḥ |

namo̍ astu̱ nīla̍grīvāya sahasrā̱kṣāya̍ mī̱ḍhuṣe̎ |

atho̱ ye a̍sya̱ sattvā̍no̱'haṃ tebhyo̍'kara̱nnama̍ḥ |

pramu̍ñca̱ dhanva̍na̱stvamu̱bhayo̱rārtni̍yo̱rjyām |

yāśca̍ te̱ hasta̱ iṣa̍va̱ḥ parā̱ tā bha̍gavo vapa |

a̱va̱tatya̱ dhanu̱stvagṃ saha̍srākṣa̱ śate̍ṣudhe |

ni̱śīrya̍ śa̱lyānā̱ṃ mukhā̍ śi̱vo na̍ḥ su̱manā̍ bhava |

vijya̱ṃ dhanu̍ḥ kapa̱rdino̱ viśa̍lyo̱ bāṇa̍vāgṃ u̱ta |

ane̍śanna̱syeṣa̍va ā̱bhura̍sya niṣa̱ṅgathi̍ḥ |

yā te̍ he̱tirmī̍ḍhuṣṭama̱ haste̍ ba̱bhūva̍ te̱ dhanu̍ḥ |

tayā̱'smān, vi̱śvata̱stvama̍ya̱kṣmayā̱ pari̍bbhuja |

nama̍ste a̱stvāyu̍dhā̱yānā̍tatāya dhṛ̱ṣṇave̎ |

u̱bhābhyā̍mu̱ta te̱ namo̍ bā̱hubhyā̱ṃ tava̱ dhanva̍ne |

pari̍ te̱ dhanva̍no he̱tira̱smānvṛ̍ṇaktu vi̱śvata̍ḥ |

atho̱ ya i̍ṣu̱dhistavā̱re a̱smannidhe̍hi̱ tam || 1 ||

śambha̍ve̱ nama̍ḥ |

nama̍ste astu bhagavanviśveśva̱rāya̍ mahāde̱vāya̍ tryamba̱kāya̍

tripurānta̱kāya̍ trikāgnikā̱lāya̍ kālāgniru̱drāya̍ nīlaka̱ṇṭhāya̍

mṛtyuñja̱yāya̍ sarveśva̱rāya̍ sadāśi̱vāya̍ śrīmanmahāde̱vāya̱ nama̍ḥ ||

namo̱ hira̍ṇyabāhave senā̱nye̍ di̱śāṃ ca̱ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ vṛ̱kṣebhyo̱ hari̍keśebhyaḥ paśū̱nāṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ sa̱spiñja̍rāya̱ tviṣī̍mate pathī̱nāṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ babhlu̱śāya̍ vivyā̱dhine'nnā̍nā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̱ hari̍keśāyopavī̱tine̍ pu̱ṣṭānā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ bha̱vasya̍ he̱tyai jaga̍tā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ ru̱drāyā̍tatā̱vine̱ kṣetrā̍ṇā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ sū̱tāyāha̍ntyāya̱ vanā̍nā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̱ rohi̍tāya stha̱pata̍ye vṛ̱kṣāṇā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ ma̱ntriṇe̍ vāṇi̱jāya̱ kakṣā̍ṇā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ bhuva̱ntaye̍ vārivaskṛ̱tāyauṣa̍dhīnā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ u̱ccairgho̍ṣāyākra̱ndaya̍te pattī̱nāṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ kṛtsnavī̱tāya̱ dhāva̍te̱ sattva̍nā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ nama̍ḥ || 2 ||

nama̱ḥ saha̍mānāya nivyā̱dhina̍ āvyā̱dhinī̍nā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ kaku̱bhāya̍ niṣa̱ṅgiṇe̎ ste̱nānā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ niṣa̱ṅgiṇa̍ iṣudhi̱mate̱ taska̍rāṇā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̱ vañca̍te pari̱vañca̍te stāyū̱nāṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍ nice̱rave̍ parica̱rāyāra̍ṇyānā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ sṛkā̱vibhyo̱ jighāgṃ̍sadbhyo muṣṇa̱tāṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

namo̍'si̱madbhyo̱ nakta̱ñcara̍dbhyaḥ prakṛ̱ntānā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̍ uṣṇī̱ṣiṇe̍ girica̱rāya̍ kulu̱ñcānā̱ṃ pata̍ye̱ namo̱

nama̱ iṣu̍madbhyo dhanvā̱vibhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ ātanvā̱nebhya̍ḥ prati̱dadhā̍nebhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ ā̱yaccha̍dbhyo visṛ̱jadbhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

namo'sya̍dbhyo̱ vidhya̍dbhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱ āsī̍nebhya̱ḥ śayā̍nebhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ sva̱padbhyo̱ jāgra̍dbhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱stiṣṭha̍dbhyo̱ dhāva̍dbhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ sa̱bhābhya̍ḥ sa̱bhāpa̍tibhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

namo̱ aśve̱bhyo'śva̍patibhyaśca vo̱ nama̍ḥ || 3 ||

nama̍ āvyā̱dhinī̎bhyo vi̱vidhya̍ntībhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱ uga̍ṇābhyastṛgṃha̱tībhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

namo̍ gṛ̱tsebhyo̍ gṛ̱tsapa̍tibhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

namo̱ vrāte̎bhyo̱ vrāta̍patibhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

namo̍ ga̱ṇebhyo̍ ga̱ṇapa̍tibhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

namo̱ virū̍pebhyo vi̱śvarū̍pebhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

namo̍ ma̱hadbhya̍ḥ, kṣulla̱kebhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

namo̍ ra̱thibhyo̍'ra̱thebhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱ namo̱ rathe̎bhyo̱ ratha̍patibhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱ḥ senā̎bhyaḥ senā̱nibhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ, kṣa̱ttṛbhya̍ḥ saṅgrahī̱tṛbhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱stakṣa̍bhyo rathakā̱rebhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱ḥ kulā̍lebhyaḥ ka̱rmāre̎bhyaśca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ḥ pu̱ñjiṣṭe̎bhyo niṣā̱debhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̍ iṣu̱kṛdbhyo̍ dhanva̱kṛdbhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

namo̍ mṛga̱yubhya̍ḥ śva̱nibhya̍śca vo̱ namo̱

nama̱ḥ śvabhya̱ḥ śvapa̍tibhyaśca vo̱ nama̍ḥ || 4 ||

namo̍ bha̱vāya̍ ca ru̱drāya̍ ca̱ nama̍ḥ śa̱rvāya̍ ca paśu̱pata̍ye ca̱

namo̱ nīla̍grīvāya ca śiti̱kaṇṭhā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ kapa̱rdine̍ ca̱ vyu̍ptakeśāya ca̱

nama̍ḥ sahasrā̱kṣāya̍ ca śa̱tadha̍nvane ca̱ namo̍ giri̱śāya̍ ca śipivi̱ṣṭāya̍ ca̱

namo̍ mī̱ḍhuṣṭa̍māya̱ ceṣu̍mate ca̱ namo̎ hra̱svāya̍ ca vāma̱nāya̍ ca̱

namo̍ bṛha̱te ca̱ varṣī̍yase ca̱ namo̍ vṛ̱ddhāya̍ ca sa̱ṃvṛdhva̍ne ca̱

namo̱ agri̍yāya ca pratha̱māya̍ ca̱ nama̍ ā̱śave̍ cāji̱rāya̍ ca̱

nama̱ḥ śīghri̍yāya ca̱ śībhyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ ū̱rmyā̍ya cāvasva̱nyā̍ya ca̱

nama̍ḥ srota̱syā̍ya ca̱ dvīpyā̍ya ca || 5 ||

namo̎ jye̱ṣṭhāya̍ ca kani̱ṣṭhāya̍ ca̱ nama̍ḥ pūrva̱jāya̍ cāpara̱jāya̍ ca̱

namo̍ madhya̱māya̍ cāpaga̱lbhāya̍ ca̱ namo̍ jagha̱nyā̍ya ca̱ budhni̍yāya ca̱

nama̍ḥ so̱bhyā̍ya ca pratisa̱ryā̍ya ca̱ namo̱ yāmyā̍ya ca̱ kṣemyā̍ya ca̱

nama̍ urva̱ryā̍ya ca̱ khalyā̍ya ca̱ nama̱ḥ ślokyā̍ya cā'vasā̱nyā̍ya ca̱

namo̱ vanyā̍ya ca̱ kakṣyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ śra̱vāya̍ ca pratiśra̱vāya̍ ca̱

nama̍ ā̱śuṣe̍ṇāya cā̱śura̍thāya ca̱ nama̱ḥ śūrā̍ya cāvabhinda̱te ca̱

namo̍ va̱rmiṇe̍ ca varū̱thine̍ ca̱ namo̍ bi̱lmine̍ ca kava̱cine̍ ca̱

nama̍ḥ śru̱tāya̍ ca śrutase̱nāya̍ ca || 6 ||

namo̍ dundu̱bhyā̍ya cāhana̱nyā̍ya ca̱ namo̍ dhṛ̱ṣṇave̍ ca pramṛ̱śāya̍ ca̱

namo̍ dū̱tāya̍ ca̱ prahi̍tāya ca̱ namo̍ niṣa̱ṅgiṇe̍ ceṣudhi̱mate̍ ca̱

nama̍stī̱kṣṇeṣa̍ve cāyu̱dhine̍ ca̱ nama̍ḥ svāyu̱dhāya̍ ca su̱dhanva̍ne ca̱

nama̱ḥ srutyā̍ya ca̱ pathyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ kā̱ṭyā̍ya ca nī̱pyā̍ya ca̱

nama̱ḥ sūdyā̍ya ca sara̱syā̍ya ca̱ namo̍ nā̱dyāya̍ ca vaiśa̱ntāya̍ ca̱

nama̱ḥ kūpyā̍ya cāva̱ṭyā̍ya ca̱ namo̱ varṣyā̍ya cāva̱rṣyāya̍ ca̱

namo̍ me̱ghyā̍ya ca vidyu̱tyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ ī̱dhriyā̍ya cāta̱pyā̍ya ca̱

namo̱ vātyā̍ya ca̱ reṣmi̍yāya ca̱ namo̍ vāsta̱vyā̍ya ca vāstu̱ pāya̍ ca || 7 ||

nama̱ḥ somā̍ya ca ru̱drāya̍ ca̱ nama̍stā̱mrāya̍ cāru̱ṇāya̍ ca̱

nama̍ḥ śa̱ṅgāya̍ ca paśu̱pata̍ye ca̱ nama̍ u̱grāya̍ ca bhī̱māya̍ ca̱

namo̍ agreva̱dhāya̍ ca dūreva̱dhāya̍ ca̱ namo̍ ha̱ntre ca̱ hanī̍yase ca̱

namo̍ vṛ̱kṣebhyo̱ hari̍keśebhyo̱ nama̍stā̱rāya̱ nama̍śśa̱mbhave̍ ca mayo̱bhave̍ ca̱

nama̍ḥ śaṅka̱rāya̍ ca mayaska̱rāya̍ ca̱ nama̍ḥ śi̱vāya̍ ca śi̱vata̍rāya ca̱

nama̱stīrthyā̍ya ca̱ kūlyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ pā̱ryā̍ya cāvā̱ryā̍ya ca̱

nama̍ḥ pra̱tara̍ṇāya co̱ttara̍ṇāya ca̱ nama̍ ātā̱ryā̍ya cālā̱dyā̍ya ca̱

nama̱ḥ śaṣpyā̍ya ca̱ phenyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ sika̱tyā̍ya ca pravā̱hyā̍ya ca || 8 ||

nama̍ iri̱ṇyā̍ya ca prapa̱thyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ kigṃśi̱lāya̍ ca̱ kṣaya̍ṇāya ca̱

nama̍ḥ kapa̱rdine̍ ca pula̱staye̍ ca̱ namo̱ goṣṭhyā̍ya ca̱ gṛhyā̍ya ca̱

nama̱stalpyā̍ya ca̱ gehyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ kā̱ṭyā̍ya ca gahvare̱ṣṭhāya̍ ca̱

namo̎ hrada̱yyā̍ya ca nive̱ṣpyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ pāgṃ sa̱vyā̍ya ca raja̱syā̍ya ca̱

nama̱ḥ śuṣkyā̍ya ca hari̱tyā̍ya ca̱ namo̱ lopyā̍ya cola̱pyā̍ya ca̱

nama̍ ū̱rvyā̍ya ca sū̱rmyā̍ya ca̱ nama̍ḥ pa̱rṇyā̍ya ca parṇaśa̱dyā̍ya ca̱

namo̍'pagu̱ramā̍ṇāya cābhighna̱te ca̱ nama̍ ākhkhida̱te ca̍ prakhkhida̱te ca̱

namo̍ vaḥ kiri̱kebhyo̍ de̱vānā̱gṃ̱ hṛda̍yebhyo̱ namo̍ vikṣīṇa̱kebhyo̱ namo̍

vicinva̱tkebhyo̱ nama̍ ānirha̱tebhyo̱ nama̍ āmīva̱tkebhya̍ḥ || 9 ||

drāpe̱ andha̍saspate̱ dari̍dra̱nnīla̍lohita |

e̱ṣāṃ puru̍ṣāṇāme̱ṣāṃ pa̍śū̱nāṃ mā bhermā'ro̱ mo e̍ṣā̱ṃ kiñca̱nāma̍mat |

yā te̍ rudra śi̱vā ta̱nūḥ śi̱vā vi̱śvāha̍bheṣajī |

śi̱vā ru̱drasya̍ bheṣa̱jī tayā̍ no mṛḍa jī̱vase̎ |

i̱māgṃ ru̱drāya̍ ta̱vase̍ kapa̱rdine̎ kṣa̱yadvī̍rāya̱ prabha̍rāmahe ma̱tim |

yathā̍ na̱ḥ śamasa̍ddvi̱pade̱ catu̍ṣpade̱ viśva̍ṃ pu̱ṣṭaṃ grāme̍

a̱sminnanā̍turam |

mṛ̱ḍā no̍ rudro̱ta no̱ maya̍skṛdhi kṣa̱yadvī̍rāya̱ nama̍sā vidhema te |

yacchaṃ ca̱ yośca̱ manu̍rāya̱je pi̱tā tada̍śyāma̱ tava̍ rudra̱ praṇī̍tau |

mā no̍ ma̱hānta̍mu̱ta mā no̍ arbha̱kaṃ mā na̱ ukṣa̍ntamu̱ta mā na̍ ukṣi̱tam |

mā no̍'vadhīḥ pi̱tara̱ṃ mota mā̱tara̍ṃ pri̱yā mā na̍sta̱nuvo̍ rudra rīriṣaḥ |

mā na̍sto̱ke tana̍ye̱ mā na̱ āyu̍ṣi̱ mā no̱ goṣu̱ mā no̱ aśve̍ṣu rīriṣaḥ |

vī̱rānmā no̍ rudra bhāmi̱to'va̍dhīrha̱viṣma̍nto̱ nama̍sā vidhema te |

ā̱rātte̍ go̱ghna u̱ta pū̍ruṣa̱ghne kṣa̱yadvī̍rāya su̱mnama̱sme te̍ astu |

rakṣā̍ ca no̱ adhi̍ ca deva brū̱hyadhā̍ ca na̱ḥ śarma̍ yaccha dvi̱barhā̎ḥ |

stu̱hi śru̱taṃ ga̍rta̱sada̱ṃ yuvā̍naṃ mṛ̱ganna bhī̱mamu̍paha̱tnumu̱gram |

mṛ̱ḍā ja̍ri̱tre ru̍dra̱ stavā̍no a̱nyante̍ a̱smanniva̍pantu̱ senā̎ḥ |

pari̍ṇo ru̱drasya̍ he̱tirvṛ̍ṇaktu̱ pari̍ tve̱ṣasya̍ durma̱ti ra̍ghā̱yoḥ |

ava̍ sthi̱rā ma̱ghava̍dbhyastanuṣva̱ mīḍhva̍sto̱kāya̱ tana̍yāya mṛḍaya |

mīḍhu̍ṣṭama̱ śiva̍tama śi̱vo na̍ḥ su̱manā̍ bhava |

pa̱ra̱me vṛ̱kṣa āyu̍dhanni̱dhāya̱ kṛtti̱ṃ vasā̍na̱ āca̍ra̱ pinā̍ka̱ṃ bibhra̱dāga̍hi |

viki̍rida̱ vilo̍hita̱ nama̍ste astu bhagavaḥ |

yāste̍ sa̱hasragṃ̍ he̱tayo̱nyama̱smanniva̍pantu̱ tāḥ |

sa̱hasrā̍ṇi sahasra̱dhā bā̍hu̱vostava̍ he̱taya̍ḥ |

tāsā̱mīśā̍no bhagavaḥ parā̱cīnā̱ mukhā̍ kṛdhi || 10 ||

sa̱hasrā̍ṇi sahasra̱śo ye ru̱drā adhi̱ bhūmyā̎m |

teṣāgṃ̍ sahasrayoja̱ne'va̱dhanvā̍ni tanmasi |

a̱sminma̍ha̱tya̍rṇa̱ve̎'ntari̍kṣe bha̱vā adhi̍ |

nīla̍grīvāḥ śiti̱kaṇṭhā̎ḥ śa̱rvā a̱dhaḥ, kṣa̍māca̱rāḥ |

nīla̍grīvāḥ śiti̱kaṇṭhā̱ divagṃ̍ ru̱drā upa̍śritāḥ |

ye vṛ̱kṣeṣu̍ sa̱spiñja̍rā̱ nīla̍grīvā̱ vilo̍hitāḥ |

ye bhū̱tānā̱madhi̍patayo viśi̱khāsa̍ḥ kapa̱rdina̍ḥ |

ye anne̍ṣu vi̱vidhya̍nti̱ pātre̍ṣu̱ piba̍to̱ janān̍ |

ye pa̱thāṃ pa̍thi̱rakṣa̍ya ailabṛ̱dā ya̱vyudha̍ḥ |

ye tī̱rthāni̍ pra̱cara̍nti sṛ̱kāva̍nto niṣa̱ṅgiṇa̍ḥ |

ya e̱tāva̍ntaśca̱ bhūyāgṃ̍saśca̱ diśo̍ ru̱drā vi̍tasthi̱re |

teṣāgṃ̍ sahasrayoja̱ne'va̱dhanvā̍ni tanmasi |

namo̍ ru̱drebhyo̱ ye pṛ̍thi̱vyāṃ ye̎'ntari̍kṣe̱ ye

di̱vi yeṣā̱manna̱ṃ vāto̍ va̱ṟṣamiṣa̍va̱stebhyo̱ daśa̱ prācī̱rdaśa̍ dakṣi̱ṇā daśa̍

pra̱tīcī̱rdaśodī̍cī̱rdaśo̱rdhvāstebhyo̱ nama̱ste no̍ mṛḍayantu̱ te yaṃ

dvi̱ṣmo yaśca̍ no̱ dveṣṭi̱ taṃ vo̱ jambhe̍ dadhāmi || 11 ||

trya̍mbakaṃ yajāmahe suga̱ndhiṃ pu̍ṣṭi̱vardha̍nam |

u̱rvā̱ru̱kami̍va̱ bandha̍nānmṛ̱tyormu̍kṣīya̱ mā'mṛtā̎t |

yo ru̱dro a̱gnau yo a̱psu ya oṣa̍dhīṣu̱ yo ru̱dro viśvā̱ bhuva̍nā vi̱veśa̱

tasmai̍ ru̱drāya̱ namo̍ astu |

tamu̍ ṣṭu̱hi̱ yaḥ svi̱ṣuḥ su̱dhanvā̱ yo viśva̍sya̱ kṣaya̍ti bheṣa̱jasya̍ |

yakṣvā̎ma̱he sau̎mana̱sāya̍ ru̱draṃ namo̎bhirde̱vamasu̍raṃ duvasya |

a̱yaṃ me̱ hasto̱ bhaga̍vāna̱yaṃ me̱ bhaga̍vattaraḥ |

a̱yaṃ me̎ vi̱śvabhe̎ṣajo̱'yagṃ śi̱vābhi̍marśanaḥ |

ye te̍ sa̱hasra̍ma̱yuta̱ṃ pāśā̱ mṛtyo̱ martyā̍ya̱ hanta̍ve |

tān ya̱jñasya̍ mā̱yayā̱ sarvā̱nava̍ yajāmahe |

mṛ̱tyave̱ svāhā̍ mṛ̱tyave̱ svāhā̎ |

om̐ namo bhagavate rudrāya viṣṇave mṛtyu̍rme pā̱hi ||

prāṇānāṃ granthirasi rudro mā̍ viśā̱ntakaḥ |

tenānnenā̎pyāya̱sva | sadāśi̱vom ||

||

om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

|| camakapraśnaḥ ||

om̐ agnā̍viṣṇū sa̱joṣa̍se̱mā va̍rdhantu vā̱ṃ gira̍ḥ |

dyu̱mnairvāje̍bhi̱rāga̍tam ||

vāja̍śca me prasa̱vaśca̍ me̱ praya̍tiśca me̱ prasi̍tiśca me dhī̱tiśca̍ me̱

kratu̍śca me̱ svara̍śca me̱ śloka̍śca me śrā̱vaśca̍ me̱ śruti̍śca me̱

jyoti̍śca me̱ suva̍śca me prā̱ṇaśca̍ me'pā̱naśca̍ me

vyā̱naśca̱ me'su̍śca me ci̱ttaṃ ca̍ ma̱ ādhī̍taṃ ca me̱ vākca̍ me̱ mana̍śca me̱

cakṣu̍śca me̱ śrotra̍ṃ ca me̱ dakṣa̍śca me̱ bala̍ṃ ca ma̱ oja̍śca me̱ saha̍śca ma̱

āyu̍śca me ja̱rā ca̍ ma ā̱tmā ca̍ me ta̱nūśca̍ me̱ śarma̍ ca me̱

varma̍ ca me̱'ṅgā̍ni ca me̱'sthāni̍ ca me̱ parūgṃ̍ṣi ca me̱

śarī̍rāṇi ca me || 1 ||

jyaiṣṭhya̍ṃ ca ma̱ ādhi̍patyaṃ ca me ma̱nyuśca̍ me̱

bhāma̍śca̱ me'ma̍śca̱ me'mbha̍śca me je̱mā ca̍ me mahi̱mā ca̍ me vari̱mā ca̍ me

prathi̱mā ca̍ me va̱rṣmā ca̍ me drāghu̱yā ca̍ me vṛ̱ddhaṃ ca̍ me̱

vṛddhi̍śca me sa̱tyaṃ ca̍ me śra̱ddhā ca̍ me̱ jaga̍cca me̱ dhana̍ṃ ca me̱

vaśa̍śca me̱ tviṣi̍śca me krī̱ḍā ca̍ me̱ moda̍śca me jā̱taṃ ca̍ me

jani̱ṣyamā̍ṇaṃ ca me sū̱ktaṃ ca̍ me sukṛ̱taṃ ca̍ me vi̱ttaṃ ca̍ me̱

vedya̍ṃ ca me bhū̱taṃ ca̍ me bhavi̱ṣyacca̍ me su̱gaṃ ca̍ me su̱patha̍ṃ ca ma

ṛ̱ddhaṃ ca̍ ma̱ ṛddhi̍śca me kḷ̱ptaṃ ca̍ me̱ kḷpti̍śca me ma̱tiśca̍ me

suma̱tiśca̍ me || 2 ||

śaṃ ca̍ me̱ maya̍śca me pri̱yaṃ ca̍ me'nukā̱maśca̍ me̱ kāma̍śca me saumana̱saśca̍ me

bha̱draṃ ca̍ me̱ śreya̍śca me̱ vasya̍śca me̱ yaśa̍śca me̱ bhaga̍śca me̱

dravi̍ṇaṃ ca me ya̱ntā ca̍ me dha̱rtā ca̍ me̱ kṣema̍śca me̱ dhṛti̍śca me̱

viśva̍ṃ ca me̱ maha̍śca me sa̱ṃvicca̍ me̱ jñātra̍ṃ ca me̱ sūśca̍ me

pra̱sūśca̍ me̱ sīra̍ṃ ca me la̱yaśca̍ ma

ṛ̱taṃ ca̍ me̱'mṛta̍ṃ ca me'ya̱kṣmaṃ ca̱ me'nā̍mayacca me jī̱vātu̍śca me

dīrghāyu̱tvaṃ ca̍ me'nami̱traṃ ca̱ me'bha̍yaṃ ca me su̱gaṃ ca̍ me̱ śaya̍naṃ ca me

sū̱ṣā ca̍ me su̱dina̍ṃ ca me || 3 ||

ūrkca̍ me sū̱nṛtā̍ ca me̱ paya̍śca me̱ rasa̍śca me ghṛ̱taṃ ca̍ me̱ madhu̍ ca me̱

sagdhi̍śca me̱ sapī̍tiśca me kṛ̱ṣiśca̍ me̱ vṛṣṭi̍śca me̱ jaitra̍ṃ ca ma̱

audbhi̍dyaṃ ca me ra̱yiśca̍ me̱ rāya̍śca me pu̱ṣṭaṃ ca̍ me̱ puṣṭi̍śca me

vi̱bhu ca̍ me pra̱bhu ca̍ me ba̱hu ca̍ me̱ bhūya̍śca me pū̱rṇaṃ ca̍ me

pū̱rṇata̍raṃ ca̱ me'kṣi̍tiśca me̱ kūya̍vāśca̱ me'nna̍ṃ ca̱ me'kṣu̍cca me

vrī̱haya̍śca me̱ yavā̎śca me̱ māṣā̎śca me̱ tilā̎śca me mu̱dgāśca̍ me

kha̱lvā̎śca me go̱dhūmā̎śca me ma̱surā̎śca me pri̱yaṅga̍vaśca̱ me'ṇa̍vaśca me

śyā̱mākā̎śca me nī̱vārā̎śca me || 4 ||

aśmā̍ ca me̱ mṛtti̍kā ca me gi̱raya̍śca me̱ parva̍tāśca me̱ sika̍tāśca me̱

vana̱spata̍yaśca me̱ hira̍ṇyaṃ ca̱ me'ya̍śca me̱ sīsa̍ṃ ca me̱ trapu̍śca me

śyā̱maṃ ca̍ me lo̱haṃ ca̍ me̱'gniśca̍ ma̱ āpa̍śca me vī̱rudha̍śca ma̱ oṣa̍dhayaśca me

kṛṣṭapa̱cyaṃ ca̍ me'kṛṣṭapa̱cyaṃ ca̍ me grā̱myāśca̍ me

pa̱śava̍ āra̱ṇyāśca̍ ya̱jñena̍ kalpantāṃ vi̱ttaṃ ca me̱ vitti̍śca me

bhū̱taṃ ca̍ me̱ bhūti̍śca me̱ vasu̍ ca me vasa̱tiśca̍ me̱ karma̍ ca me̱

śakti̍śca̱ me'rtha̍śca ma̱ ema̍śca ma̱ iti̍śca me̱ gati̍śca me || 5 ||

a̱gniśca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me̱ soma̍śca ma̱ indra̍śca me savi̱tā ca̍ ma̱

indra̍śca me̱ sara̍svatī ca ma̱ indra̍śca me pū̱ṣā ca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me̱

bṛha̱spati̍śca ma̱ indra̍śca me mi̱traśca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me̱ varu̍ṇaśca ma̱

indra̍śca me̱ tvaṣṭā̍ ca ma̱ indra̍śca me dhā̱tā ca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me̱

viṣṇu̍śca ma̱ indra̍śca me̱'śvinau̍ ca ma̱ indra̍śca me ma̱ruta̍śca ma̱

indra̍śca me̱ viśve̍ ca me de̱vā indra̍śca me pṛthi̱vī ca̍ ma̱

indra̍śca me̱'ntari̍kṣaṃ ca ma̱ indra̍śca me̱ dyauśca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me̱

diśa̍śca ma̱ indra̍śca me mū̱rdhā ca̍ ma̱ indra̍śca me pra̱jāpa̍tiśca ma̱

indra̍śca me || 6 ||

a̱gṃ̱śuśca̍ me ra̱śmiśca̱ me'dā̎bhyaśca̱ me'dhi̍patiśca ma

upā̱gṃ̱śuśca̍ me'ntaryā̱maśca̍ ma aindravāya̱vaśca̍ me maitrāvaru̱ṇaśca̍ ma

āśvi̱naśca̍ me pratipra̱sthāna̍śca me śu̱kraśca̍ me ma̱nthī ca̍ ma

āgraya̱ṇaśca̍ me vaiśvade̱vaśca̍ me dhru̱vaśca̍ me vaiśvāna̱raśca̍ ma

ṛtugra̱hāśca̍ me'tigrā̱hyā̎śca ma aindrā̱gnaśca̍ me vaiśvade̱vaśca̍ me

marutva̱tīyā̎śca me māhe̱ndraśca̍ ma ādi̱tyaśca̍ me sāvi̱traśca̍ me

sārasva̱taśca̍ me pau̱ṣṇaśca̍ me pātnīva̱taśca̍ me hāriyoja̱naśca̍ me || 7 ||

i̱dhmaśca̍ me ba̱rhiśca̍ me̱ vedi̍śca me̱ dhiṣṇi̍yāśca me̱ sruca̍śca me

cama̱sāśca̍ me̱ grāvā̍ṇaśca me̱ svara̍vaśca ma upara̱vāśca̍ me'dhi̱ṣava̍ṇe ca me

droṇakala̱śaśca̍ me vāya̱vyā̍ni ca me pūta̱bhṛcca̍ ma ādhava̱nīya̍śca ma̱

āgnī̎dhraṃ ca me havi̱rdhāna̍ṃ ca me gṛ̱hāśca̍ me̱ sada̍śca me

puro̱ḍāśā̎śca me paca̱tāśca̍ me'vabhṛ̱thaśca̍ me svagākā̱raśca̍ me || 8 ||

a̱gniśca̍ me gha̱rmaśca̍ me̱'rkaśca̍ me̱ sūrya̍śca me

prā̱ṇaśca̍ me'śvame̱dhaśca̍ me pṛthi̱vī ca̱ me'di̍tiśca me̱ diti̍śca me̱

dyauśca̍ me̱ śakkva̍rīra̱ṅgula̍yo̱ diśa̍śca me ya̱jñena̍ kalpantā̱mṛkca̍ me̱

sāma̍ ca me̱ stoma̍śca me̱ yaju̍śca me dī̱kṣā ca̍ me̱ tapa̍śca ma ṛ̱tuśca̍ me

vra̱taṃ ca̍ me'horā̱trayo̎rvṛ̱ṣṭyā bṛ̍hadrathanta̱re ca̍ me

ya̱jñena̍ kalpetām || 9 ||

garbhā̎śca me va̱tsāśca̍ me̱ tryavi̍śca me trya̱vī ca̍ me

ditya̱vāṭ ca̍ me dityau̱hī ca̍ me̱ pañcā̍viśca me pañcā̱vī ca̍ me

triva̱tsaśca̍ me triva̱tsā ca̍ me turya̱vāṭ ca̍ me turyau̱hī ca̍ me

paṣṭha̱vāṭ ca̍ me paṣṭhau̱hī ca̍ ma u̱kṣā ca̍ me va̱śā ca̍ ma ṛṣa̱bhaśca̍ me

ve̱hacca̍ me'na̱ḍvāñca̍ me dhe̱nuśca̍ ma̱

āyu̍rya̱jñena̍ kalpatāṃ prā̱ṇo ya̱jñena̍ kalpatāmapā̱no ya̱jñena̍ kalpatāṃ

vyā̱no ya̱jñena̍ kalpatā̱ṃ cakṣu̍rya̱jñena̍ kalpatā̱g̱

śrotra̍ṃ ya̱jñena̍ kalpatā̱ṃ mano̍ ya̱jñena̍ kalpatā̱ṃ

vāgya̱jñena̍ kalpatāmā̱tmā ya̱jñena̍ kalpatāṃ

ya̱jño ya̱jñena̍ kalpatām || 10 ||

ekā̍ ca me ti̱sraśca̍ me̱ pañca̍ ca me sa̱pta ca̍ me̱ nava̍ ca ma̱ ekā̍daśa ca me̱

trayo̍daśa ca me̱ pañca̍daśa ca me sa̱ptada̍śa ca me̱ nava̍daśa ca ma̱ eka̍vigṃśatiśca me̱

trayo̍vigṃśatiśca me̱ pañca̍vigṃśatiśca me sa̱ptavigṃ̍śatiśca me̱

nava̍vigṃśatiśca ma̱ eka̍trigṃśacca me̱ traya̍strigṃśacca me̱

cata̍sraśca me̱'ṣṭau ca̍ me̱ dvāda̍śa ca me̱ ṣoḍa̍śa ca me vigṃśa̱tiśca̍ me̱

catu̍rvigṃśatiśca me̱'ṣṭāvigṃ̍śatiśca me̱ dvātrigṃ̍śacca me̱

ṣaṭtrigṃ̍śacca me catvāri̱gṃ̱śacca̍ me̱

catu̍ścatvārigṃśacca me̱'ṣṭāca̍tvārigṃśacca me̱

vāja̍śca prasa̱vaścā̍pi̱jaśca̱ kratu̍śca̱ suva̍śca mū̱rdhā ca̱

vyaśni̍yaścāntyāya̱naścāntya̍śca bhauva̱naśca̱

bhuva̍na̱ścādhi̍patiśca || 11 ||

om̐ iḍā̍ deva̱hūrmanu̍ryajña̱nīrbṛha̱spati̍rukthāma̱dāni̍

śagṃsiṣa̱dviśve̍de̱vāḥ sū̎kta̱vāca̱ḥ pṛthi̍vīmāta̱rmā mā̍ higṃsī̱rmadhu̍

maniṣye̱ madhu̍ janiṣye̱ madhu̍ vakṣyāmi̱ madhu̍ vadiṣyāmi̱ madhu̍matīṃ

de̱vebhyo̱ vāca̍mudyāsagṃ śuśrū̱ṣeṇyā̎ṃ manu̱ṣye̎bhya̱staṃ mā̍ de̱vā

a̍vantu śo̱bhāyai̍ pi̱taro'nu̍madantu ||

om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

Krishna Yajurveda, Taittiriya Samhita ↗

### vedic-chant-durga-suktam
6. Durgā Sūktam

om̐ jā̱tave̍dase sunavāma̱ soma̍ marātīya̱to nida̍hāti̱ veda̍ḥ |

sa na̍ḥ parṣa̱dati̍ du̱rgāṇi̱ viśvā̍ nā̱veva̱ sindhu̍ṃ duri̱tā'tya̱gniḥ ||

tāma̱gniva̍rṇā̱ṃ tapa̍sā jvala̱ntīṃ vai̍roca̱nīṃ ka̍rmapha̱leṣu̱ juṣṭā̎m |

du̱rgāṃ de̱vīgṃ śara̍ṇama̱haṃ prapa̍dye su̱tara̍si tarase̱ nama̍ḥ ||

agne̱ tvaṃ pā̍rayā̱ navyo̍ a̱smānthsva̱stibhi̱rati̍ du̱rgāṇi̱ viśvā̎ |

pūśca̍ pṛ̱thvī ba̍hu̱lā na̍ u̱rvī bhavā̍ to̱kāya̱ tana̍yāya̱ śaṃyoḥ ||

viśvā̍ni no du̱rgahā̍ jātaveda̱ḥ sindhu̱nna nā̱vā du̍ri̱tā'ti̍parṣi |

agne̍ atri̱vanmana̍sā gṛṇā̱no̎'smāka̍ṃ bodhyavi̱tā ta̱nūnā̎m ||

pṛ̱ta̱nā̱ jita̱gṃ̱ saha̍mānamu̱grama̱gnigṃ hu̍vema para̱māthsa̱dhasthā̎t |

sa na̍ḥ parṣa̱dati̍ du̱rgāṇi̱ viśvā̱ kṣāma̍dde̱vo ati̍ duri̱tā'tya̱gniḥ ||

pra̱tnoṣi̍ ka̱mīḍyo̍ adhva̱reṣu̍ sa̱nācca̱ hotā̱ navya̍śca̱ satsi̍ |

svāñcā̎'gne ta̱nuva̍ṃ pi̱praya̍svā̱smabhya̍ṃ ca̱ saubha̍ga̱māya̍jasva ||

gobhi̱rjuṣṭa̍ma̱yujo̱ niṣi̍kta̱ṃ tave̎ndra viṣṇo̱ranu̱sañca̍rema |

nāka̍sya pṛ̱ṣṭhama̱bhi sa̱ṃvasā̍no̱ vaiṣṇa̍vīṃ lo̱ka i̱ha mā̍dayantām ||

om̐ kā̱tyā̱ya̱nāya̍ vi̱dmahe̍ kanyaku̱māri̍ dhīmahi |

tanno̍ durgiḥ praco̱dayā̎t ||

|| om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

Krishna Yajurveda, Taittiriya Aranyaka (Mahanarayana Upanishad)

### vedic-chant-shivopasana-mantra
7. Śivopāsanamantrāḥ

om̐ nidha̍napataye̱ namaḥ | nidha̍napatāntikāya̱ namaḥ |

ūrdhvāya̱ namaḥ | ūrdhvaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

hiraṇyāya̱ namaḥ | hiraṇyaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

suvarṇāya̱ namaḥ | suvarṇaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

divyāya̱ namaḥ | divyaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

bhavāya̱ namaḥ | bhavaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

śarvāya̱ namaḥ | śarvaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

śivāya̱ namaḥ | śivaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

jvalāya̱ namaḥ | jvalaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

ātmāya̱ namaḥ | ātmaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

paramāya̱ namaḥ | paramaliṅgāya̱ namaḥ |

etatsomasya̍ sūrya̱sya̱ sarvaliṅgaggṃ̍ sthāpa̱ya̱ti̱ pāṇimantra̍ṃ pavi̱tram ||

sa̱dyojā̱taṃ pra̍padyā̱mi sa̱dyojā̱tāya̱ vai namo̱ nama̍ḥ |

bha̱ve bha̍ve̱ nāti̍bhave bhavasva̱ mām |

bha̱vodbha̍vāya̱ nama̍ḥ ||

vā̱ma̱de̱vāya̱ namo̎ jye̱ṣṭhāya̱ nama̍śśre̱ṣṭhāya̱ namo̍ ru̱drāya̱ nama̱ḥ

kālā̍ya̱ nama̱ḥ kala̍vikaraṇāya̱ namo̱ bala̍vikaraṇāya̱ namo̱ balā̍ya̱ namo̱

bala̍pramathanāya̱ nama̱ssarva̍bhūtadamanāya̱ namo̍ ma̱nonma̍nāya̱ nama̍ḥ ||

a̱ghore̎bhyo'tha̱ ghore̎bhyo̱ ghora̱ghora̍tarebhyaḥ |

sarve̎bhyaḥ sarva̱śarve̎bhyo̱ nama̍ste astu ru̱drarū̍pebhyaḥ ||

tatpuru̍ṣāya vi̱dmahe̍ mahāde̱vāya̍ dhīmahi |

tanno̍ rudraḥ praco̱dayā̎t ||

īśānassarva̍vidyā̱nā̱mīśvarassarva̍bhūtā̱nā̱ṃ

brahmā'dhi̍pati̱rbrahma̱ṇo'dhi̍pati̱rbrahmā̍ śi̱vo me̍ astu sadāśi̱vom ||

namo hiraṇyabāhave hiraṇyavarṇāya hiraṇyarūpāya

hiraṇyapataye'mbikāpataya umāpataye paśupataye̍ namo̱ namaḥ |

ṛ̱tagṃ sa̱tyaṃ pa̍raṃ bra̱hma̱ pu̱ruṣa̍ṃ kṛṣṇa̱piṅga̍lam |

ū̱rdhvare̍taṃ vi̍rūpā̱kṣa̱ṃ vi̱śvarū̍pāya̱ vai namo̱ nama̍ḥ ||

sarvo̱ vai ru̱drastasmai̍ ru̱drāya̱ namo̍ astu |

puru̍ṣo̱ vai ru̱drassanma̱ho namo̱ nama̍ḥ |

viśva̍ṃ bhū̱taṃ bhuva̍naṃ ci̱traṃ ba̍hu̱dhā jā̱taṃ jāya̍mānaṃ ca̱ yat |

sarvo̱ hye̍ṣa ru̱drastasmai̍ ru̱drāya̱ namo̍ astu |

kadru̱drāya̱ prace̍tase mī̱ḍhuṣṭa̍māya̱ tavya̍se |

vo̱cema̱ śanta̍magṃ hṛ̱de |

sarvo̱ hye̍ṣa ru̱drastasmai̍ ru̱drāya̱ namo̍ astu ||

om̐ śānti̱ḥ śānti̱ḥ śānti̍ḥ ||

Krishna Yajurveda, Taittiriya Aranyaka (Mahanarayana Upanishad) ↗

### vedic-chant-pavamana-suktam
8. Pavamānasūktam

|| pavamānasūktam ||

śrī gurubhyo namaḥ | hariḥ om ||

gaṇānā̎ṃ tvā ga̱ṇapa̍tiṃ havāmahe ka̱viṃ ka̍vī̱nāmu̍pa̱maśra̍vastamam |

jye̱ṣṭha̱rāja̱ṃ brahma̍ṇāṃ brahmaṇaspata̱ ā na̍ḥ śṛ̱ṇvannū̱tibhi̍ḥ sīda̱ sāda̍nam ||

śrī mahāgaṇapataye namaḥ ||

pra ṇo̎ de̱vī sara̍svatī̱ vāje̎bhirvā̱jinī̎vatī |

dhī̱nāma̍vi̱trya̍vatu ||

śrī mahāsarasvatyai namaḥ ||

veda̱ vāta̍sya varta̱nimu̱rorṛ̱ṣvasya̍ bṛha̱taḥ |

vedā̱ ye a̱dhyāsa̍te ||

śrī vedapuruṣāya namaḥ ||

śa̱tadhā̎ra̱mutsa̱makṣī̎yamāṇaṃ vipa̱ścita̎ṃ pi̱tara̱ṃ vaktvā̎nām |

me̱ḷiṃ mada̎ntaṃ pi̱troru̱pasthe̱ taṃ ro̎dasī pipṛtaṃ satya̱vāca̎m ||

ṛ̱co a̱kṣare̎ para̱me vyo̎ma̱n yasmi̎n de̱vā adhi̱ viśve̎ niṣe̱duḥ |

yastanna veda̱ kimṛ̱cā ka̍riṣyati̱ ya ittadvi̱dusta i̱me samā̎sate ||

|| atha pavamānasūktam ||

Prathama Adhyaya

svādi̍ṣṭhayā̱ madi̍ṣṭhayā̱ pava̍sva soma̱ dhāra̍yā |

indrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve su̱taḥ || 9.001.01 ||

ra̱kṣo̱hā vi̱śvaca̍rṣaṇira̱bhi yoni̱mayo̎hatam |

druṇā̎ sa̱dhastha̱māsa̍dat || 9.001.02 ||

va̱ri̱vo̱dhāta̍mo bhava̱ maṃhi̍ṣṭho vṛtra̱hanta̍maḥ |

parṣi̱ rādho̎ ma̱ghonā̎m || 9.001.03 ||

a̱bhya̍rṣa ma̱hānā̎ṃ de̱vānā̎ṃ vī̱timandha̍sā |

a̱bhi vāja̍mu̱ta śrava̍ḥ || 9.001.04 ||

tvāmacchā̎ carāmasi̱ tadidartha̎ṃ di̱vedi̍ve |

indo̱ tve na̍ ā̱śasa̍ḥ || 9.001.05 ||

pu̱nāti̍ te pari̱sruta̱ṃ soma̱ṃ sūrya̍sya duhi̱tā |

vāre̎ṇa̱ śaśva̍tā̱ tanā̎ || 9.001.06 ||

tamī̱maṇvī̎ sama̱rya ā gṛ̱bhṇanti̱ yoṣa̍ṇo̱ daśa̍ |

svasā̎ra̱ḥ pārye̎ di̱vi || 9.001.07 ||

tamī̎ṃ hinvantya̱gruvo̱ dhama̎nti bāku̱raṃ dṛtim̎ |

tri̱dhātu̍ vāra̱ṇaṃ madhu̍ || 9.001.08 ||

a̱bhī3̱̍ mamaghnyā̎ u̱ta śrī̱ṇanti̍ dhe̱nava̱ḥ śiśum̎ |

soma̱mindrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve || 9.001.09 ||

a̱syedindro̱ made̱ṣvā viśvā̎ vṛ̱trāṇi̍ jighnate |

śūro̎ ma̱ghā ca̍ maṃhate || 9.001.10 ||

pava̍sva deva̱vīrati̍ pa̱vitra̎ṃ soma̱ raṃhyā̎ |

indra̍mindo̱ vṛṣā vi̍śa || 9.002.01 ||

ā va̍cyasva̱ mahi̱ psaro̱ vṛṣe̎ndo dyu̱mnava̍ttamaḥ |

ā yoni̎ṃ dharṇa̱siḥ sa̍daḥ || 9.002.02 ||

adhu̍kṣata pri̱yaṃ madhu̱ dhārā̎ su̱tasya̍ ve̱dhasa̍ḥ |

a̱po va̍siṣṭa su̱kratu̍ḥ || 9.002.03 ||

ma̱hānta̎ṃ tvā ma̱hīranvāpo̎ arṣanti̱ sindha̍vaḥ |

yadgobhi̍rvāsayi̱ṣyase̎ || 9.002.04 ||

sa̱mu̱dro a̱psu mā̎mṛje viṣṭa̱mbho dha̱ruṇo̎ di̱vaḥ |

soma̍ pa̱vitre̎ asma̱yuḥ || 9.002.05 ||

aci̍krada̱dvṛṣā̱ hari̍rma̱hānmi̱tro na da̍rśa̱taḥ |

saṃ sūrye̎ṇa rocate || 9.002.06 ||

gira̍sta inda̱ oja̍sā marmṛ̱jyante̎ apa̱syuva̍ḥ |

yābhi̱rmadā̎ya̱ śumbha̍se || 9.002.07 ||

taṃ tvā̱ madā̎ya̱ ghṛṣva̍ya ulokakṛ̱tnumī̎mahe |

tava̱ praśa̍stayo ma̱hīḥ || 9.002.08 ||

a̱smabhya̍mindavindra̱yurmadhva̍ pavasva̱ dhāra̍yā |

pa̱rjanyo̎ vṛṣṭi̱māṃ i̍va || 9.002.09 ||

go̱ṣā i̎ndo nṛ̱ṣā a̍syaśva̱sā vā̎ja̱sā u̱ta |

ā̱tmā ya̱jñasya̍ pū̱rvyaḥ || 9.002.10 ||

e̱ṣa de̱vo ama̍rtyaḥ parṇa̱vīri̍va dīyati |

a̱bhi droṇā̎nyā̱sadam̎ || 9.003.01 ||

e̱ṣa de̱vo vi̱pā kṛ̱to'ti̱ hvarā̎ṃsi dhāvati |

pava̍māno̱ adā̎bhyaḥ || 9.003.02 ||

e̱ṣa de̱vo vi̍pa̱nyubhi̱ pava̍māna ṛtā̱yubhi̍ḥ |

hari̱rvājā̎ya mṛjyate || 9.003.03 ||

e̱ṣa viśvā̎ni̱ vāryā̱ śūro̱ yanni̍va̱ satva̍bhiḥ |

pava̍mānaḥ siṣāsati || 9.003.04 ||

e̱ṣa de̱vo ra̍tharyati̱ pava̍māno daśasyati |

ā̱viṣkṛ̍ṇoti vagva̱num || 9.003.05 ||

e̱ṣa viprai̎ra̱bhiṣṭu̍to̱'po de̱vo vi gā̎hate |

dadha̱dratnā̎ni dā̱śuṣe̎ || 9.003.06 ||

e̱ṣa diva̱ṃ vi dhā̎vati ti̱ro rajā̎ṃsi̱ dhāra̍yā |

pava̍māna̱ kani̍kradat || 9.003.07 ||

e̱ṣa diva̱ṃ vyāsa̍ratti̱ro rajā̱ṃsyaspṛ̍taḥ |

pava̍mānaḥ svadhva̱raḥ || 9.003.08 ||

e̱ṣa pra̱tnena̱ janma̍nā de̱vo de̱vebhya̍ su̱taḥ |

hari̍ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati || 9.003.09 ||

e̱ṣa u̱ sya pu̍ruvra̱to ja̍jñā̱no ja̱naya̱nniṣa̍ḥ |

dhāra̍yā pavate su̱taḥ || 9.003.10 ||

sanā̎ ca soma̱ jeṣi̍ ca̱ pava̍māna̱ mahi̱ śrava̍ḥ |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.01 ||

sanā̱ jyoti̱ sanā̱ sva1̱̍ rviśvā̎ ca soma̱ saubha̍gā |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.02 ||

sanā̱ dakṣa̍mu̱ta kratu̱mapa̍ soma̱ mṛdho̎ jahi |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.03 ||

pavī̎tāraḥ punī̱tana̱ soma̱mindrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.04 ||

tvaṃ sūrye̎ na̱ ā bha̍ja̱ tava̱ kratvā̱ tavo̱tibhi̍ḥ |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.05 ||

tava̱ kratvā̱ tavo̱tibhi̱rjyokpa̍śyema̱ sūryam̎ |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.06 ||

a̱bhya̍rṣa svāyudha̱ soma̍ dvi̱barha̍saṃ ra̱yim |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.07 ||

a̱bhya1̱̍ rṣāna̍pacyuto ra̱yiṃ sa̱matsu̍ sāsa̱hiḥ |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.08 ||

tvāṃ ya̱jñaira̍vīvṛdha̱npava̍māna̱ vidha̎rmaṇi |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.09 ||

ra̱yiṃ na̍ści̱trama̱śvina̱mindo̎ vi̱śvāyu̱mā bha̍ra |

athā̎ no̱ vasya̍saskṛdhi || 9.004.10 ||

sami̍ddho vi̱śvata̱spati̱ pava̍māno̱ vi rā̎jati |

prī̱ṇanvṛṣā̱ kani̍kradat || 9.005.01 ||

tanū̱napā̱tpava̍māna̱ḥ śṛṅge̱ śiśā̎no arṣati |

a̱ntari̍kṣeṇa̱ rāra̍jat || 9.005.02 ||

ī̱ḷenya̱ḥ pava̍māno ra̱yirvi rā̎jati dyu̱mān |

madho̱rdhārā̎bhi̱roja̍sā || 9.005.03 ||

ba̱rhiḥ prā̱cīna̱moja̍sā̱ pava̍mānaḥ stṛ̱ṇanhari̍ḥ |

de̱veṣu̍ de̱va ī̎yate || 9.005.04 ||

udātai̎rjihate bṛ̱had‌dvāro̎ de̱vīrhi̍ra̱ṇyayī̎ḥ |

pava̍mānena̱ suṣṭu̍tāḥ || 9.005.05 ||

su̱śi̱lpe bṛ̍ha̱tī ma̱hī pava̍māno vṛṣaṇyati |

nakto̱ṣāsā̱ na da̍rśa̱te || 9.005.06 ||

u̱bhā de̱vā nṛ̱cakṣa̍sā̱ hotā̎rā̱ daivyā̎ huve |

pava̍māna̱ indro̱ vṛṣā̎ || 9.005.07 ||

bhāra̍tī̱ pava̍mānasya̱ sara̍sva̱tīḷā̎ ma̱hī |

i̱maṃ no̎ ya̱jñamā ga̍manti̱sro de̱vīḥ su̱peśa̍saḥ || 9.005.08 ||

tvaṣṭā̎ramagra̱jāṃ go̱pāṃ pu̍ro̱yāvā̎na̱mā hu̍ve |

indu̱rindro̱ vṛṣā̱ hari̱ḥ pava̍mānaḥ pra̱jāpa̍tiḥ || 9.005.09 ||

vana̱spati̎ṃ pavamāna̱ madhvā̱ sama̎ṅgdhi̱ dhāra̍yā |

sa̱hasra̍valśa̱ṃ hari̍ta̱ṃ bhrāja̍mānaṃ hira̱ṇyayam̎ || 9.005.10 ||

viśve̎ devā̱ svāhā̎kṛti̱ṃ pava̍māna̱syā ga̍ta |

vā̱yurbṛha̱spati̱ sūryo̱'gnirindra̍ sa̱joṣa̍saḥ || 9.005.11

ma̱ndrayā̎ soma̱ dhāra̍yā̱ vṛṣā̎ pavasva deva̱yuḥ |

avyo̱ vāre̎ṣvasma̱yuḥ || 9.006.01 ||

a̱bhi tyaṃ madya̱ṃ mada̱minda̱vindra̱ iti̍ kṣara |

a̱bhi vā̱jino̱ arva̍taḥ || 9.006.02 ||

a̱bhi tyaṃ pū̱rvyaṃ mada̎ṃ suvā̱no a̍rṣa pa̱vitra̱ ā |

a̱bhi vāja̍mu̱ta śrava̍ḥ || 9.006.03 ||

anu̍ dra̱psāsa̱ inda̍va̱ āpo̱ na pra̱vatā̎saran |

pu̱nā̱nā indra̍māśata || 9.006.04 ||

yamatya̍miva vā̱jina̎ṃ mṛ̱janti̱ yoṣa̍ṇo̱ daśa̍ |

vane̱ krīḷa̎nta̱matya̍vim || 9.006.05 ||

taṃ gobhi̱rvṛṣa̍ṇa̱ṃ rasa̱ṃ madā̎ya de̱vavī̎taye |

su̱taṃ bharā̎ya̱ saṃ sṛ̍ja || 9.006.06 ||

de̱vo de̱vāya̱ dhāra̱yendrā̎ya pavate su̱taḥ |

payo̱ yada̍sya pī̱paya̍t || 9.006.07 ||

ā̱tmā ya̱jñasya̱ raṃhyā̎ suṣvā̱ṇaḥ pa̍vate su̱taḥ |

pra̱tnaṃ ni pā̎ti̱ kāvyam̎ || 9.006.08 ||

e̱vā pu̍nā̱na i̎ndra̱yurmada̎ṃ madiṣṭha vī̱taye̎ |

guhā̎ ciddadhiṣe̱ gira̍ḥ || 9.006.09 ||

asṛ̍gra̱minda̍vaḥ pa̱thā dharma̎nnṛ̱tasya̍ su̱śriya̍ḥ |

vi̱dā̱nā a̍sya̱ yoja̍nam || 9.007.01 ||

pra dhārā̱ madhvo̎ agri̱yo ma̱hīra̱po vi gā̎hate |

ha̱virha̱viṣṣu̱ vandya̍ḥ || 9.007.02 ||

pra yu̱jo vā̱co a̍gri̱yo vṛṣāva̍ cakrada̱dvane̎ |

sadmā̱bhi sa̱tyo a̍dhva̱raḥ || 9.007.03 ||

pari̱ yatkāvyā̎ ka̱virnṛ̱mṇā vasā̎no̱ arṣa̍ti |

sva̍rvā̱jī si̍ṣāsati || 9.007.04 ||

pava̍māno a̱bhi spṛdho̱ viśo̱ rāje̎va sīdati |

yadī̎mṛ̱ṇvanti̍ ve̱dhasa̍ḥ || 9.007.05 ||

avyo̱ vāre̱ pari̍ pri̱yo hari̱rvane̎ṣu sīdati |

re̱bho va̍nuṣyate ma̱tī || 9.007.06 ||

sa vā̱yumindra̍ma̱śvinā̎ sā̱kaṃ made̎na gacchati |

raṇā̱ yo a̍sya̱ dharma̍bhiḥ || 9.007.07 ||

ā mi̱trāvaru̍ṇā̱ bhaga̱ṃ madhva̍ pavanta ū̱rmaya̍ḥ |

vi̱dā̱nā a̍sya̱ śakma̍bhiḥ || 9.007.08 ||

a̱smabhya̎ṃ rodasī ra̱yiṃ madhvo̱ vāja̍sya sā̱taye̎ |

śravo̱ vasū̎ni̱ saṃ ji̍tam || 9.007.09 ||

e̱te somā̎ a̱bhi pri̱yamindra̍sya̱ kāma̍makṣaran |

vardha̎nto asya vī̱ryam̎ || 9.008.01 ||

pu̱nā̱nāsa̍ścamū̱ṣado̱ gaccha̎nto vā̱yuma̱śvinā̎ |

te no̎ dhāntu su̱vīryam̎ || 9.008.02 ||

indra̍sya soma̱ rādha̍se punā̱no hārdi̍ codaya |

ṛ̱tasya̱ yoni̍mā̱sadam̎ || 9.008.03 ||

mṛ̱janti̍ tvā̱ daśa̱ kṣipo̎ hi̱nvanti̍ sa̱pta dhī̱taya̍ḥ |

anu̱ viprā̎ amādiṣuḥ || 9.008.04 ||

de̱vebhya̍stvā̱ madā̎ya̱ kaṃ sṛ̍jā̱namati̍ me̱ṣya̍ḥ |

saṃ gobhi̍rvāsayāmasi || 9.008.05 ||

pu̱nā̱naḥ ka̱laśe̱ṣvā vastrā̎ṇyaru̱ṣo hari̍ḥ |

pari̱ gavyā̎nyavyata || 9.008.06 ||

ma̱ghona̱ ā pa̍vasva no ja̱hi viśvā̱ apa̱ dviṣa̍ḥ |

indo̱ sakhā̎ya̱mā vi̍śa || 9.008.07 ||

vṛ̱ṣṭiṃ di̱vaḥ pari̍ srava dyu̱mnaṃ pṛ̍thi̱vyā adhi̍ |

saho̎ naḥ soma pṛ̱tsu dhā̎ḥ || 9.008.08 ||

nṛ̱cakṣa̍saṃ tvā va̱yamindra̍pītaṃ sva̱rvidam̎ |

bha̱kṣī̱mahi̍ pra̱jāmiṣam̎ || 9.008.09 ||

pari̍ pri̱yā di̱vaḥ ka̱virvayā̎ṃsi na̱ptyo̎rhi̱taḥ |

su̱vā̱no yā̎ti ka̱vikra̍tuḥ || 9.009.01 ||

prapra̱ kṣayā̎ya̱ panya̍se̱ janā̎ya̱ juṣṭo̎ a̱druhe̎ |

vī̱tya̍rṣa̱ cani̍ṣṭhayā || 9.009.02 ||

sa sū̱nurmā̱tarā̱ śuci̍rjā̱to jā̱te a̍rocayat |

ma̱hānma̱hī ṛ̍tā̱vṛdhā̎ || 9.009.03 ||

sa sa̱pta dhī̱tibhi̍rhi̱to na̱dyo̎ ajinvada̱druha̍ḥ |

yā eka̱makṣi̍ vāvṛ̱dhuḥ || 9.009.04 ||

tā a̱bhi santa̱mastṛ̍taṃ ma̱he yuvā̎na̱mā da̍dhuḥ |

indu̍mindra̱ tava̍ vra̱te || 9.009.05 ||

a̱bhi vahni̱rama̍rtyaḥ sa̱pta pa̍śyati̱ vāva̍hiḥ |

krivi̍rde̱vīra̍tarpayat || 9.009.06 ||

avā̱ kalpe̎ṣu naḥ puma̱stamā̎ṃsi soma̱ yodhyā̎ |

tāni̍ punāna jaṅghanaḥ || 9.009.07 ||

nū navya̍se̱ navī̎yase sū̱ktāya̍ sādhayā pa̱thaḥ |

pra̱tna̱vadro̎cayā̱ ruca̍ḥ || 9.009.08 ||

pava̍māna̱ mahi̱ śravo̱ gāmaśva̎ṃ rāsi vī̱rava̍t |

sanā̎ me̱dhāṃ sanā̱ sva̍ḥ || 9.009.09 ||

pra svā̱nāso̱ rathā̎ i̱vārva̎nto̱ na śra̍va̱syava̍ḥ |

somā̎so rā̱ye a̍kramuḥ || 9.010.01 ||

hi̱nvā̱nāso̱ rathā̎ iva dadhanvi̱re gabha̍styoḥ |

bharā̎saḥ kā̱riṇā̎miva || 9.010.02 ||

rājā̎no̱ na praśa̍stibhi̱ somā̎so̱ gobhi̍rañjate |

ya̱jño na sa̱pta dhā̱tṛbhi̍ḥ || 9.010.03 ||

pari̍ suvā̱nāsa̱ inda̍vo̱ madā̎ya ba̱rhaṇā̎ gi̱rā |

su̱tā a̍rṣanti̱ dhāra̍yā || 9.010.04 ||

ā̱pā̱nāso̎ vi̱vasva̍to̱ jana̎nta u̱ṣaso̱ bhagam̎ |

sūrā̱ aṇva̱ṃ vi ta̎nvate || 9.010.05 ||

apa̱ dvārā̎ matī̱nāṃ pra̱tnā ṛ̎ṇvanti kā̱rava̍ḥ |

vṛṣṇo̱ hara̍sa ā̱yava̍ḥ || 9.010.06 ||

sa̱mī̱cī̱nāsa̍ āsate̱ hotā̎raḥ sa̱ptajā̎mayaḥ |

pa̱dameka̍sya̱ pipra̍taḥ || 9.010.07 ||

nābhā̱ nābhi̎ṃ na̱ ā da̍de̱ cakṣu̍ści̱tsūrye̱ sacā̎ |

ka̱verapa̍tya̱mā du̍he || 9.010.08 ||

a̱bhi pri̱yā di̱vaspa̱dama̍dhva̱ryubhi̱rguhā̎ hi̱tam |

sūra̍ paśyati̱ cakṣa̍sā || 9.010.09 ||

upā̎smai gāyatā nara̱ pava̍mānā̱yenda̍ve |

a̱bhi de̱vāṃ iya̍kṣate || 9.011.01 ||

a̱bhi te̱ madhu̍nā̱ payo'tha̎rvāṇo aśiśrayuḥ |

de̱vaṃ de̱vāya̍ deva̱yu || 9.011.02 ||

sa na̍ pavasva̱ śaṃ gave̱ śaṃ janā̎ya̱ śamarva̍te |

śaṃ rā̎ja̱nnoṣa̍dhībhyaḥ || 9.011.03 ||

ba̱bhrave̱ nu svata̍vase'ru̱ṇāya̍ divi̱spṛśe̎ |

somā̎ya gā̱thama̍rcata || 9.011.04 ||

hasta̍cyutebhi̱radri̍bhiḥ su̱taṃ soma̎ṃ punītana |

madhā̱vā dhā̎vatā̱ madhu̍ || 9.011.05 ||

nama̱sedupa̍ sīdata da̱dhneda̱bhi śrī̎ṇītana |

indu̱mindre̎ dadhātana || 9.011.06 ||

a̱mi̱tra̱hā vica̍rṣaṇi̱ pava̍sva soma̱ śaṃ gave̎ |

de̱vebhyo̎ anukāma̱kṛt || 9.011.07 ||

indrā̎ya soma̱ pāta̍ve̱ madā̎ya̱ pari̍ ṣicyase |

ma̱na̱ścinmana̍sa̱spati̍ḥ || 9.011.08 ||

pava̍māna su̱vīrya̎ṃ ra̱yiṃ so̎ma rirīhi naḥ |

inda̱vindre̎ṇa no yu̱jā || 9.011.09

somā̎ asṛgra̱minda̍vaḥ su̱tā ṛ̱tasya̱ sāda̍ne |

indrā̎ya̱ madhu̍mattamāḥ || 9.012.01 ||

a̱bhi viprā̎ anūṣata̱ gāvo̎ va̱tsaṃ na mā̱tara̍ḥ |

indra̱ṃ soma̍sya pī̱taye̎ || 9.012.02 ||

ma̱da̱cyutkṣe̎ti̱ sāda̍ne̱ sindho̎rū̱rmā vi̍pa̱ścit |

somo̎ gau̱rī adhi̍ śri̱taḥ || 9.012.03 ||

di̱vo nābhā̎ vicakṣa̱ṇo'vyo̱ vāre̎ mahīyate |

somo̱ yaḥ su̱kratu̍ ka̱viḥ || 9.012.04 ||

yaḥ soma̍ ka̱laśe̱ṣvāṃ a̱ntaḥ pa̱vitra̱ āhi̍taḥ |

tamindu̱ pari̍ ṣasvaje || 9.012.05 ||

pra vāca̱mindu̍riṣyati samu̱drasyādhi̍ vi̱ṣṭapi̍ |

jinva̱nkośa̎ṃ madhu̱ścutam̎ || 9.012.06 ||

nitya̍stotro̱ vana̱spati̍rdhī̱nāma̱ntaḥ sa̍ba̱rdugha̍ḥ |

hi̱nvā̱no mānu̍ṣā yu̱gā || 9.012.07 ||

a̱bhi pri̱yā di̱vaspa̱dā somo̎ hinvā̱no a̍rṣati |

vipra̍sya̱ dhāra̍yā ka̱viḥ || 9.012.08 ||

ā pa̍vamāna dhāraya ra̱yiṃ sa̱hasra̍varcasam |

a̱sme i̎ndo svā̱bhuvam̎ || 9.012.09

Dvitiya Adhyaya

soma̍ punā̱no a̍rṣati sa̱hasra̍dhāro̱ atya̍viḥ |

vā̱yorindra̍sya niṣkṛ̱tam || 9.013.01 ||

pava̍mānamavasyavo̱ vipra̍ma̱bhi pra gā̎yata |

su̱ṣvā̱ṇaṃ de̱vavī̎taye || 9.013.02 ||

pava̎nte̱ vāja̍sātaye̱ somā̎ sa̱hasra̍pājasaḥ |

gṛ̱ṇā̱nā de̱vavī̎taye || 9.013.03 ||

u̱ta no̱ vāja̍sātaye̱ pava̍sva bṛha̱tīriṣa̍ḥ |

dyu̱madi̎ndo su̱vīryam̎ || 9.013.04 ||

te na̍ saha̱sriṇa̎ṃ ra̱yiṃ pava̎ntā̱mā su̱vīryam̎ |

su̱vā̱nā de̱vāsa̱ inda̍vaḥ || 9.013.05 ||

atyā̎ hiyā̱nā na he̱tṛbhi̱rasṛ̍gra̱ṃ vāja̍sātaye |

vi vāra̱mavya̍mā̱śava̍ḥ || 9.013.06 ||

vā̱śrā a̍rṣa̱ntīnda̍vo̱'bhi va̱tsaṃ na dhe̱nava̍ḥ |

da̱dha̱nvi̱re gabha̍styoḥ || 9.013.07 ||

juṣṭa̱ indrā̎ya matsa̱raḥ pava̍māna̱ kani̍kradat |

viśvā̱ apa̱ dviṣo̎ jahi || 9.013.08 ||

a̱pa̱ghnanto̱ arā̎vṇa̱ pava̍mānāḥ sva̱rdṛśa̍ḥ |

yonā̎vṛ̱tasya̍ sīdata || 9.013.09 ||

pari̱ prāsi̍ṣyadatka̱viḥ sindho̎rū̱rmāvadhi̍ śri̱taḥ |

kā̱raṃ bibhra̍tpuru̱spṛham̎ || 9.014.01 ||

gi̱rā yadī̱ saba̎ndhava̱ pañca̱ vrātā̎ apa̱syava̍ḥ |

pa̱ri̱ṣkṛ̱ṇvanti̍ dharṇa̱sim || 9.014.02 ||

āda̍sya śu̱ṣmiṇo̱ rase̱ viśve̎ de̱vā a̍matsata |

yadī̱ gobhi̍rvasā̱yate̎ || 9.014.03 ||

ni̱ri̱ṇā̱no vi dhā̎vati̱ jaha̱ccharyā̎ṇi̱ tānvā̎ |

atrā̱ saṃ ji̍ghnate yu̱jā || 9.014.04 ||

na̱ptībhi̱ryo vi̱vasva̍taḥ śu̱bhro na mā̎mṛ̱je yuvā̎ |

gāḥ kṛ̎ṇvā̱no na ni̱rṇijam̎ || 9.014.05 ||

ati̍ śri̱tī ti̍ra̱ścatā̎ ga̱vyā ji̍gā̱tyaṇvyā̎ |

va̱gnumi̍yarti̱ yaṃ vi̱de || 9.014.06 ||

a̱bhi kṣipa̱ sama̍gmata ma̱rjaya̎ntīri̱ṣaspatim̎ |

pṛ̱ṣṭhā gṛ̍bhṇata vā̱jina̍ḥ || 9.014.07 ||

pari̍ di̱vyāni̱ marmṛ̍śa̱dviśvā̎ni soma̱ pārthi̍vā |

vasū̎ni yāhyasma̱yuḥ || 9.014.08 ||

e̱ṣa dhi̱yā yā̱tyaṇvyā̱ śūro̱ rathe̎bhirā̱śubhi̍ḥ |

gaccha̱nnindra̍sya niṣkṛ̱tam || 9.015.01 ||

e̱ṣa pu̱rū dhi̍yāyate bṛha̱te de̱vatā̎taye |

yatrā̱mṛtā̎sa̱ āsa̍te || 9.015.02 ||

e̱ṣa hi̱to vi nī̍yate̱'ntaḥ śu̱bhrāva̍tā pa̱thā |

yadī̎ tu̱ñjanti̱ bhūrṇa̍yaḥ || 9.015.03 ||

e̱ṣa śṛṅgā̎ṇi̱ dodhu̍va̱cchiśī̎te yū̱thyo̱3̱̍ vṛṣā̎ |

nṛ̱mṇā dadhā̎na̱ oja̍sā || 9.015.04 ||

e̱ṣa ru̱kmibhi̍rīyate vā̱jī śu̱bhrebhi̍ra̱ṃśubhi̍ḥ |

pati̱ sindhū̎nā̱ṃ bhava̎n || 9.015.05 ||

e̱ṣa vasū̎ni pibda̱nā paru̍ṣā yayi̱vāṃ ati̍ |

ava̱ śāde̎ṣu gacchati || 9.015.06 ||

e̱taṃ mṛ̍janti̱ marjya̱mupa̱ droṇe̎ṣvā̱yava̍ḥ |

pra̱ca̱krā̱ṇaṃ ma̱hīriṣa̍ḥ || 9.015.07 ||

e̱tamu̱ tyaṃ daśa̱ kṣipo̎ mṛ̱janti̍ sa̱pta dhī̱taya̍ḥ |

svā̱yu̱dhaṃ ma̱dinta̍mam || 9.015.08 ||

pra te̎ so̱tāra̍ o̱ṇyo̱3̱̍ rasa̱ṃ madā̎ya̱ ghṛṣva̍ye |

sargo̱ na ta̱ktyeta̍śaḥ || 9.016.01 ||

kratvā̱ dakṣa̍sya ra̱thya̍ma̱po vasā̎na̱mandha̍sā |

go̱ṣāmaṇve̎ṣu saścima || 9.016.02 ||

ana̍ptama̱psu du̱ṣṭara̱ṃ soma̎ṃ pa̱vitra̱ ā sṛ̍ja |

pu̱nī̱hīndrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve || 9.016.03 ||

pra pu̍nā̱nasya̱ ceta̍sā̱ soma̍ḥ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati |

kratvā̎ sa̱dhastha̱māsa̍dat || 9.016.04 ||

pra tvā̱ namo̎bhi̱rinda̍va̱ indra̱ somā̎ asṛkṣata |

ma̱he bharā̎ya kā̱riṇa̍ḥ || 9.016.05 ||

pu̱nā̱no rū̱pe a̱vyaye̱ viśvā̱ arṣa̎nna̱bhi śriya̍ḥ |

śūro̱ na goṣu̍ tiṣṭhati || 9.016.06 ||

di̱vo na sānu̍ pi̱pyuṣī̱ dhārā̎ su̱tasya̍ ve̱dhasa̍ḥ |

vṛthā̎ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati || 9.016.07 ||

tvaṃ so̎ma vipa̱ścita̱ṃ tanā̎ punā̱na ā̱yuṣu̍ |

avyo̱ vāra̱ṃ vi dhā̎vasi || 9.016.08 ||

pra ni̱mnene̎va̱ sindha̍vo̱ ghnanto̎ vṛ̱trāṇi̱ bhūrṇa̍yaḥ |

somā̎ asṛgramā̱śava̍ḥ || 9.017.01 ||

a̱bhi su̍vā̱nāsa̱ inda̍vo vṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ pṛthi̱vīmi̍va |

indra̱ṃ somā̎so akṣaran || 9.017.02 ||

atyū̎rmirmatsa̱ro mada̱ soma̍ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati |

vi̱ghnanrakṣā̎ṃsi deva̱yuḥ || 9.017.03 ||

ā ka̱laśe̎ṣu dhāvati pa̱vitre̱ pari̍ ṣicyate |

u̱kthairya̱jñeṣu̍ vardhate || 9.017.04 ||

ati̱ trī so̎ma roca̱nā roha̱nna bhrā̎jase̱ divam̎ |

i̱ṣṇansūrya̱ṃ na co̎dayaḥ || 9.017.05 ||

a̱bhi viprā̎ anūṣata mū̱rdhanya̱jñasya̍ kā̱rava̍ḥ |

dadhā̎nā̱ścakṣa̍si pri̱yam || 9.017.06 ||

tamu̍ tvā vā̱jina̱ṃ naro̎ dhī̱bhirviprā̎ ava̱syava̍ḥ |

mṛ̱janti̍ de̱vatā̎taye || 9.017.07 ||

madho̱rdhārā̱manu̍ kṣara tī̱vraḥ sa̱dhastha̱māsa̍daḥ |

cāru̍rṛ̱tāya̍ pī̱taye̎ || 9.017.08 ||

pari̍ suvā̱no gi̍ri̱ṣṭhāḥ pa̱vitre̱ somo̎ akṣāḥ |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.01||

tvaṃ vipra̱stvaṃ ka̱virmadhu̱ pra jā̱tamandha̍saḥ |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.02||

tava̱ viśve̎ sa̱joṣa̍so de̱vāsa̍ pī̱timā̎śata |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.03 ||

ā yo viśvā̎ni̱ vāryā̱ vasū̎ni̱ hasta̍yorda̱dhe |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.04 ||

ya i̱me roda̍sī ma̱hī saṃ mā̱tare̎va̱ doha̍te |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.05 ||

pari̱ yo roda̍sī u̱bhe sa̱dyo vāje̎bhi̱rarṣa̍ti |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.06 ||

sa śu̱ṣmī ka̱laśe̱ṣvā pu̍nā̱no a̍cikradat |

made̎ṣu sarva̱dhā a̍si || 9.018.07 ||

yatso̎ma ci̱tramu̱kthya̎ṃ di̱vyaṃ pārthi̍va̱ṃ vasu̍ |

tanna̍ punā̱na ā bha̍ra || 9.019.01 ||

yu̱vaṃ hi sthaḥ sva̍rpatī̱ indra̍śca soma̱ gopa̍tī |

ī̱śā̱nā pi̍pyata̱ṃ dhiya̍ḥ || 9.019.02 ||

vṛṣā̎ punā̱na ā̱yuṣu̍ sta̱naya̱nnadhi̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ |

hari̱ sanyoni̱māsa̍dat || 9.019.03 ||

avā̎vaśanta dhī̱tayo̎ vṛṣa̱bhasyādhi̱ reta̍si |

sū̱norva̱tsasya̍ mā̱tara̍ḥ || 9.019.04 ||

ku̱vidvṛ̍ṣa̱ṇyantī̎bhyaḥ punā̱no garbha̍mā̱dadha̍t |

yāḥ śu̱kraṃ du̍ha̱te paya̍ḥ || 9.019.05 ||

upa̍ śikṣāpata̱sthuṣo̎ bhi̱yasa̱mā dhe̎hi̱ śatru̍ṣu |

pava̍māna vi̱dā ra̱yim || 9.019.06 ||

ni śatro̎ soma̱ vṛṣṇya̱ṃ ni śuṣma̱ṃ ni vaya̍stira |

dū̱re vā̎ sa̱to anti̍ vā || 9.019.07 ||

pra ka̱virde̱vavī̎ta̱ye'vyo̱ vāre̎bhirarṣati |

sā̱hvānviśvā̎ a̱bhi spṛdha̍ḥ || 9.020.01 ||

sa hi ṣmā̎ jari̱tṛbhya̱ ā vāja̱ṃ goma̎nta̱minva̍ti |

pava̍mānaḥ saha̱sriṇam̎ || 9.020.02 ||

pari̱ viśvā̎ni̱ ceta̍sā mṛ̱śase̱ pava̍se ma̱tī |

sa na̍ soma̱ śravo̎ vidaḥ || 9.020.03 ||

a̱bhya̍rṣa bṛ̱hadyaśo̎ ma̱ghava̍d‌bhyo dhru̱vaṃ ra̱yim |

iṣa̎ṃ sto̱tṛbhya̱ ā bha̍ra || 9.020.04 ||

tvaṃ rāje̎va suvra̱to gira̍ so̱mā vi̍veśitha |

pu̱nā̱no va̎hne adbhuta || 9.020.05 ||

sa vahni̍ra̱psu du̱ṣṭaro̎ mṛ̱jyamā̎no̱ gabha̍styoḥ |

soma̍śca̱mūṣu̍ sīdati || 9.020.06 ||

krī̱ḷurma̱kho na ma̎ṃha̱yuḥ pa̱vitra̎ṃ soma gacchasi |

dadha̍tsto̱tre su̱vīryam̎ || 9.020.07 ||

e̱te dhā̎va̱ntīnda̍va̱ somā̱ indrā̎ya̱ ghṛṣva̍yaḥ |

ma̱tsa̱rāsa̍ sva̱rvida̍ḥ || 9.021.01 ||

pra̱vṛ̱ṇvanto̎ abhi̱yuja̱ suṣva̍ye varivo̱vida̍ḥ |

sva̱yaṃ sto̱tre va̍ya̱skṛta̍ḥ || 9.021.02 ||

vṛthā̱ krīḷa̎nta̱ inda̍vaḥ sa̱dhastha̍ma̱bhyeka̱mit |

sindho̎rū̱rmā vya̍kṣaran || 9.021.03 ||

e̱te viśvā̎ni̱ vāryā̱ pava̍mānāsa āśata |

hi̱tā na sapta̍yo̱ rathe̎ || 9.021.04 ||

āsmi̎npi̱śaṅga̍mindavo̱ dadhā̎tā ve̱namā̱diśe̎ |

yo a̱smabhya̱marā̎vā || 9.021.05 ||

ṛ̱bhurna rathya̱ṃ nava̱ṃ dadhā̎tā̱ keta̍mā̱diśe̎ |

śu̱krāḥ pa̍vadhva̱marṇa̍sā || 9.021.06 ||

e̱ta u̱ tye a̍vīvaśa̱nkāṣṭhā̎ṃ vā̱jino̎ akrata |

sa̱taḥ prāsā̎viṣurma̱tim || 9.021.07 ||

e̱te somā̎sa ā̱śavo̱ rathā̎ iva̱ pra vā̱jina̍ḥ |

sargā̎ sṛ̱ṣṭā a̍heṣata || 9.022.01 ||

e̱te vātā̎ ivo̱rava̍ pa̱rjanya̍syeva vṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ |

a̱gneri̍va bhra̱mā vṛthā̎ || 9.022.02 ||

e̱te pū̱tā vi̍pa̱ścita̱ somā̎so̱ dadhyā̎śiraḥ |

vi̱pā vyā̎naśu̱rdhiya̍ḥ || 9.022.03 ||

e̱te mṛ̱ṣṭā ama̍rtyāḥ sasṛ̱vāṃso̱ na śa̍śramuḥ |

iya̍kṣantaḥ pa̱tho raja̍ḥ || 9.022.04 ||

e̱te pṛ̱ṣṭhāni̱ roda̍sorvipra̱yanto̱ vyā̎naśuḥ |

u̱tedamu̍tta̱maṃ raja̍ḥ || 9.022.05 ||

tantu̎ṃ tanvā̱namu̍tta̱mamanu̍ pra̱vata̍ āśata |

u̱tedamu̍tta̱māyyam̎ || 9.022.06 ||

tvaṃ so̎ma pa̱ṇibhya̱ ā vasu̱ gavyā̎ni dhārayaḥ |

ta̱taṃ tantu̍macikradaḥ || 9.022.07 ||

somā̎ asṛgramā̱śavo̱ madho̱rmada̍sya̱ dhāra̍yā |

a̱bhi viśvā̎ni̱ kāvyā̎ || 9.023.01 ||

anu̍ pra̱tnāsa̍ ā̱yava̍ pa̱daṃ navī̎yo akramuḥ |

ru̱ce ja̍nanta̱ sūryam̎ || 9.023.02 ||

ā pa̍vamāna no bharā̱ryo adā̎śuṣo̱ gayam̎ |

kṛ̱dhi pra̱jāva̍tī̱riṣa̍ḥ || 9.023.03 ||

a̱bhi somā̎sa ā̱yava̱ pava̎nte̱ madya̱ṃ madam̎ |

a̱bhi kośa̎ṃ madhu̱ścutam̎ || 9.023.04 ||

somo̎ arṣati dharṇa̱sirdadhā̎na indri̱yaṃ rasam̎ |

su̱vīro̎ abhiśasti̱pāḥ || 9.023.05 ||

indrā̎ya soma pavase de̱vebhya̍ sadha̱mādya̍ḥ |

indo̱ vāja̎ṃ siṣāsasi || 9.023.06 ||

a̱sya pī̱tvā madā̎nā̱mindro̎ vṛ̱trāṇya̍pra̱ti |

ja̱ghāna̍ ja̱ghana̍cca̱ nu || 9.023.07 ||

pra somā̎so adhanviṣu̱ pava̍mānāsa̱ inda̍vaḥ |

śrī̱ṇā̱nā a̱psu mṛ̎ñjata || 9.024.01 ||

a̱bhi gāvo̎ adhanviṣu̱rāpo̱ na pra̱vatā̎ ya̱tīḥ |

pu̱nā̱nā indra̍māśata || 9.024.02 ||

pra pa̍vamāna dhanvasi̱ somendrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve |

nṛbhi̍rya̱to vi nī̎yase || 9.024.03 ||

tvaṃ so̎ma nṛ̱māda̍na̱ pava̍sva carṣaṇī̱sahe̎ |

sasni̱ryo a̍nu̱mādya̍ḥ || 9.024.04 ||

indo̱ yadadri̍bhiḥ su̱taḥ pa̱vitra̎ṃ pari̱dhāva̍si |

ara̱mindra̍sya̱ dhāmne̎ || 9.024.05 ||

pava̍sva vṛtrahantamo̱kthebhi̍ranu̱mādya̍ḥ |

śuci̍ pāva̱ko adbhu̍taḥ || 9.024.06 ||

śuci̍ pāva̱ka u̍cyate̱ soma̍ su̱tasya̱ madhva̍ḥ |

de̱vā̱vīra̍ghaśaṃsa̱hā || 9.024.07 ||

pava̍sva dakṣa̱sādha̍no de̱vebhya̍ pī̱taye̎ hare |

ma̱rud‌bhyo̎ vā̱yave̱ mada̍ḥ || 9.025.01 ||

pava̍māna dhi̱yā hi̱to̱̍3̱'bhi yoni̱ṃ kani̍kradat |

dharma̍ṇā vā̱yumā vi̍śa || 9.025.02 ||

saṃ de̱vaiḥ śo̎bhate̱ vṛṣā̎ ka̱viryonā̱vadhi̍ pri̱yaḥ |

vṛ̱tra̱hā de̎va̱vīta̍maḥ || 9.025.03 ||

viśvā̎ rū̱pāṇyā̎vi̱śanpu̍nā̱no yā̎ti harya̱taḥ |

yatrā̱mṛtā̎sa̱ āsa̍te || 9.025.04 ||

a̱ru̱ṣo ja̱naya̱ngira̱ soma̍ pavata āyu̱ṣak |

indra̱ṃ gaccha̎nka̱vikra̍tuḥ || 9.025.05 ||

ā pa̍vasva madintama pa̱vitra̱ṃ dhāra̍yā kave |

a̱rkasya̱ yoni̍mā̱sadam̎ || 9.025.06

tama̍mṛkṣanta vā̱jina̍mu̱pasthe̱ adi̍te̱radhi̍ |

viprā̎so̱ aṇvyā̎ dhi̱yā || 9.026.01 ||

taṃ gāvo̎ a̱bhya̍nūṣata sa̱hasra̍dhāra̱makṣi̍tam |

indu̎ṃ dha̱rtāra̱mā di̱vaḥ || 9.026.02 ||

taṃ ve̱dhāṃ me̱dhayā̎hya̱npava̍māna̱madhi̱ dyavi̍ |

dha̱rṇa̱siṃ bhūri̍dhāyasam || 9.026.03 ||

tama̍hyanbhu̱rijo̎rdhi̱yā sa̱ṃvasā̎naṃ vi̱vasva̍taḥ |

pati̎ṃ vā̱co adā̎bhyam || 9.026.04 ||

taṃ sānā̱vadhi̍ jā̱mayo̱ hari̎ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

ha̱rya̱taṃ bhūri̍cakṣasam || 9.026.05 ||

taṃ tvā̎ hinvanti ve̱dhasa̱ pava̍māna girā̱vṛdham̎ |

inda̱vindrā̎ya matsa̱ram || 9.026.06 ||

e̱ṣa ka̱vira̱bhiṣṭu̍taḥ pa̱vitre̱ adhi̍ tośate |

pu̱nā̱no ghnannapa̱ sridha̍ḥ || 9.027.01 ||

e̱ṣa indrā̎ya vā̱yave̎ sva̱rjitpari̍ ṣicyate |

pa̱vitre̎ dakṣa̱sādha̍naḥ || 9.027.02 ||

e̱ṣa nṛbhi̱rvi nī̎yate di̱vo mū̱rdhā vṛṣā̎ su̱taḥ |

somo̱ vane̎ṣu viśva̱vit || 9.027.03 ||

e̱ṣa ga̱vyura̍cikrada̱tpava̍māno hiraṇya̱yuḥ |

indu̍ḥ satrā̱jidastṛ̍taḥ || 9.027.04 ||

e̱ṣa sūrye̎ṇa hāsate̱ pava̍māno̱ adhi̱ dyavi̍ |

pa̱vitre̎ matsa̱ro mada̍ḥ || 9.027.05 ||

e̱ṣa śu̱ṣmya̍siṣyadada̱ntari̍kṣe̱ vṛṣā̱ hari̍ḥ |

pu̱nā̱na indu̱rindra̱mā || 9.027.06 ||

e̱ṣa vā̱jī hi̱to nṛbhi̍rviśva̱vinmana̍sa̱spati̍ḥ |

avyo̱ vāra̱ṃ vi dhā̎vati || 9.028.01 ||

e̱ṣa pa̱vitre̎ akṣara̱tsomo̎ de̱vebhya̍ su̱taḥ |

viśvā̱ dhāmā̎nyāvi̱śan || 9.028.02 ||

e̱ṣa de̱vaḥ śu̍bhāya̱te'dhi̱ yonā̱vama̍rtyaḥ |

vṛ̱tra̱hā de̎va̱vīta̍maḥ || 9.028.03 ||

e̱ṣa vṛṣā̱ kani̍kradadda̱śabhi̍rjā̱mibhi̍rya̱taḥ |

a̱bhi droṇā̎ni dhāvati || 9.028.04 ||

e̱ṣa sūrya̍marocaya̱tpava̍māno̱ vica̍rṣaṇiḥ |

viśvā̱ dhāmā̎ni viśva̱vit || 9.028.05 ||

e̱ṣa śu̱ṣmyadā̎bhya̱ soma̍ punā̱no a̍rṣati |

de̱vā̱vīra̍ghaśaṃsa̱hā || 9.028.06 ||

prāsya̱ dhārā̎ akṣara̱nvṛṣṇa̍ su̱tasyauja̍sā |

de̱vāṃ anu̍ pra̱bhūṣa̍taḥ || 9.029.01 ||

sapti̎ṃ mṛjanti ve̱dhaso̎ gṛ̱ṇanta̍ kā̱ravo̎ gi̱rā |

jyoti̍rjajñā̱namu̱kthyam̎ || 9.029.02 ||

su̱ṣahā̎ soma̱ tāni̍ te punā̱nāya̍ prabhūvaso |

vardhā̎ samu̱dramu̱kthya̍m || 9.029.03 ||

viśvā̱ vasū̎ni sa̱ñjaya̱npava̍sva soma̱ dhāra̍yā |

i̱nu dveṣā̎ṃsi sa̱dhrya̍k || 9.029.04 ||

rakṣā̱ su no̱ ara̍ruṣaḥ sva̱nātsa̍masya̱ kasya̍ cit |

ni̱do yatra̍ mumu̱cmahe̎ || 9.029.05 ||

endo̱ pārthi̍vaṃ ra̱yiṃ di̱vyaṃ pa̍vasva̱ dhāra̍yā |

dyu̱manta̱ṃ śuṣma̱mā bha̍ra || 9.029.06 ||

pra dhārā̎ asya śu̱ṣmiṇo̱ vṛthā̎ pa̱vitre̎ akṣaran |

pu̱nā̱no vāca̍miṣyati || 9.030.01 ||

indu̍rhiyā̱naḥ so̱tṛbhi̍rmṛ̱jyamā̎na̱ḥ kani̍kradat |

iya̍rti va̱gnumi̎ndri̱yam || 9.030.02 ||

ā na̱ śuṣma̎ṃ nṛ̱ṣāhya̎ṃ vī̱rava̎ntaṃ puru̱spṛham̎ |

pava̍sva soma̱ dhāra̍yā || 9.030.03 ||

pra somo̱ ati̱ dhāra̍yā̱ pava̍māno asiṣyadat |

a̱bhi droṇā̎nyā̱sadam̎ || 9.030.04 ||

a̱psu tvā̱ madhu̍mattama̱ṃ hari̎ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

inda̱vindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.030.05 ||

su̱notā̱ madhu̍mattama̱ṃ soma̱mindrā̎ya va̱jriṇe̎ |

cāru̱ṃ śardhā̎ya matsa̱ram || 9.030.06 ||

pra somā̎saḥ svā̱dhya1̱̍ḥ pava̍mānāso akramuḥ |

ra̱yiṃ kṛ̎ṇvanti̱ ceta̍nam || 9.031.01 ||

di̱vaspṛ̍thi̱vyā adhi̱ bhave̎ndo dyumna̱vardha̍naḥ |

bhavā̱ vājā̎nā̱ṃ pati̍ḥ || 9.031.02 ||

tubhya̱ṃ vātā̎ abhi̱priya̱stubhya̍marṣanti̱ sindha̍vaḥ |

soma̱ vardha̎nti te̱ maha̍ḥ || 9.031.03 ||

ā pyā̎yasva̱ same̎tu te vi̱śvata̎ soma̱ vṛṣṇyam̎ |

bhavā̱ vāja̍sya saṅga̱the || 9.031.04 ||

tubhya̱ṃ gāvo̎ ghṛ̱taṃ payo̱ babhro̎ dudu̱hre akṣi̍tam |

varṣi̍ṣṭhe̱ adhi̱ sāna̍vi || 9.031.05 ||

svā̱yu̱dhasya̍ te sa̱to bhuva̍nasya pate va̱yam |

indo̎ sakhi̱tvamu̍śmasi || 9.031.06 ||

pra somā̎so mada̱cyuta̱ śrava̍se no ma̱ghona̍ḥ |

su̱tā vi̱dathe̎ akramuḥ || 9.032.01 ||

ādī̎ṃ tri̱tasya̱ yoṣa̍ṇo̱ hari̎ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

indu̱mindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.032.02 ||

ādī̎ṃ ha̱ṃso yathā̎ ga̱ṇaṃ viśva̍syāvīvaśanma̱tim |

atyo̱ na gobhi̍rajyate || 9.032.03 ||

u̱bhe so̎māva̱cāka̍śanmṛ̱go na ta̱kto a̍rṣasi |

sīda̎nnṛ̱tasya̱ yoni̱mā || 9.032.04 ||

a̱bhi gāvo̎ anūṣata̱ yoṣā̎ jā̱rami̍va pri̱yam |

aga̎nnā̱jiṃ yathā̎ hi̱tam || 9.032.05 ||

a̱sme dhe̎hi dyu̱madyaśo̎ ma̱ghava̍d‌bhyaśca̱ mahya̎ṃ ca |

sa̱niṃ me̱dhāmu̱ta śrava̍ḥ || 9.032.06 ||

pra somā̎so vipa̱ścito̱'pāṃ na ya̍ntyū̱rmaya̍ḥ |

vanā̎ni mahi̱ṣā i̍va || 9.033.01 ||

a̱bhi droṇā̎ni ba̱bhrava̍ śu̱krā ṛ̱tasya̱ dhāra̍yā |

vāja̱ṃ goma̎ntamakṣaran || 9.033.02 ||

su̱tā indrā̎ya vā̱yave̱ varu̍ṇāya ma̱rud‌bhya̍ḥ |

somā̎ arṣanti̱ viṣṇa̍ve || 9.033.03 ||

ti̱sro vāca̱ udī̎rate̱ gāvo̎ mimanti dhe̱nava̍ḥ |

hari̍reti̱ kani̍kradat || 9.033.04 ||

a̱bhi brahmī̎ranūṣata ya̱hvīrṛ̱tasya̍ mā̱tara̍ḥ |

ma̱rmṛ̱jyante̎ di̱vaḥ śiśum̎ || 9.033.05 ||

rā̱yaḥ sa̍mu̱drāṃśca̱turo̱'smabhya̎ṃ soma vi̱śvata̍ḥ |

ā pa̍vasva saha̱sriṇa̍ḥ || 9.033.06 ||

pra su̍vā̱no dhāra̍yā̱ tanendu̍rhinvā̱no a̍rṣati |

ru̱jaddṛ̱ḷhā vyoja̍sā || 9.034.01 ||

su̱ta indrā̎ya vā̱yave̱ varu̍ṇāya ma̱rud‌bhya̍ḥ |

somo̎ arṣati̱ viṣṇa̍ve || 9.034.02 ||

vṛṣā̎ṇa̱ṃ vṛṣa̍bhirya̱taṃ su̱nvanti̱ soma̱madri̍bhiḥ |

du̱hanti̱ śakma̍nā̱ paya̍ḥ || 9.034.03 ||

bhuva̍ttri̱tasya̱ marjyo̱ bhuva̱dindrā̎ya matsa̱raḥ |

saṃ rū̱paira̍jyate̱ hari̍ḥ || 9.034.04 ||

a̱bhīmṛ̱tasya̍ vi̱ṣṭapa̎ṃ duha̱te pṛśni̍mātaraḥ |

cāru̍ pri̱yata̍maṃ ha̱viḥ || 9.034.05 ||

same̎na̱mahru̍tā i̱mā giro̎ arṣanti sa̱sruta̍ḥ |

dhe̱nūrvā̱śro a̍vīvaśat || 9.034.06 ||

ā na̍ pavasva̱ dhāra̍yā̱ pava̍māna ra̱yiṃ pṛ̱thum |

yayā̱ jyoti̍rvi̱dāsi̍ naḥ || 9.035.01 ||

indo̎ samudramīṅkhaya̱ pava̍sva viśvamejaya |

rā̱yo dha̱rtā na̱ oja̍sā || 9.035.02 ||

tvayā̎ vī̱reṇa̍ vīravo̱'bhi ṣyā̎ma pṛtanya̱taḥ |

kṣarā̎ ṇo a̱bhi vāryam̎ || 9.035.03 ||

pra vāja̱mindu̍riṣyati̱ siṣā̎sanvāja̱sā ṛṣi̍ḥ |

vra̱tā vi̍dā̱na āyu̍dhā || 9.035.04 ||

taṃ gī̱rbhirvā̎camīṅkha̱yaṃ pu̍nā̱naṃ vā̎sayāmasi |

soma̱ṃ jana̍sya̱ gopa̍tim || 9.035.05 ||

viśvo̱ yasya̍ vra̱te jano̎ dā̱dhāra̱ dharma̍ṇa̱spate̎ḥ |

pu̱nā̱nasya̍ pra̱bhūva̍soḥ || 9.035.06 ||

asa̍rji̱ rathyo̎ yathā pa̱vitre̎ ca̱mvo̎ su̱taḥ |

kārṣma̎nvā̱jī nya̍kramīt || 9.036.01 ||

sa vahni̍ soma̱ jāgṛ̍vi̱ pava̍sva deva̱vīrati̍ |

a̱bhi kośa̎ṃ madhu̱ścutam̎ || 9.036.02 ||

sa no̱ jyotī̎ṃṣi pūrvya̱ pava̍māna̱ vi ro̎caya |

kratve̱ dakṣā̎ya no hinu || 9.036.03 ||

śu̱mbhamā̎na ṛtā̱yubhi̍rmṛ̱jyamā̎no̱ gabha̍styoḥ |

pava̍te̱ vāre̎ a̱vyaye̎ || 9.036.04 ||

sa viśvā̎ dā̱śuṣe̱ vasu̱ somo̎ di̱vyāni̱ pārthi̍vā |

pava̍tā̱māntari̍kṣyā || 9.036.05 ||

ā di̱vaspṛ̱ṣṭhama̍śva̱yurga̍vya̱yuḥ so̎ma rohasi |

vī̱ra̱yuḥ śa̍vasaspate || 9.036.06 ||

sa su̱taḥ pī̱taye̱ vṛṣā̱ soma̍ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati |

vi̱ghnanrakṣā̎ṃsi deva̱yuḥ || 9.037.01 ||

sa pa̱vitre̎ vicakṣa̱ṇo hari̍rarṣati dharṇa̱siḥ |

a̱bhi yoni̱ṃ kani̍kradat || 9.037.02 ||

sa vā̱jī ro̎ca̱nā di̱vaḥ pava̍māno̱ vi dhā̎vati |

ra̱kṣo̱hā vāra̍ma̱vyayam̎ || 9.037.03 ||

sa tri̱tasyādhi̱ sāna̍vi̱ pava̍māno arocayat |

jā̱mibhi̱ sūrya̎ṃ sa̱ha || 9.037.04 ||

sa vṛ̍tra̱hā vṛṣā̎ su̱to va̍rivo̱vidadā̎bhyaḥ |

somo̱ vāja̍mivāsarat || 9.037.05 ||

sa de̱vaḥ ka̱vine̎ṣi̱to̱3̱̍'bhi droṇā̎ni dhāvati |

indu̱rindrā̎ya ma̱ṃhanā̎ || 9.037.06 ||

e̱ṣa u̱ sya vṛṣā̱ ratho'vyo̱ vāre̎bhirarṣati |

gaccha̱nvāja̎ṃ saha̱sriṇam̎ || 9.038.01 ||

e̱taṃ tri̱tasya̱ yoṣa̍ṇo̱ hari̎ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

indu̱mindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.038.02 ||

e̱taṃ tyaṃ ha̱rito̱ daśa̍ marmṛ̱jyante̎ apa̱syuva̍ḥ |

yābhi̱rmadā̎ya̱ śumbha̍te || 9.038.03 ||

e̱ṣa sya mānu̍ṣī̱ṣvā śye̱no na vi̱kṣu sī̎dati |

gaccha̎ñjā̱ro na yo̱ṣitam̎ || 9.038.04 ||

e̱ṣa sya madyo̱ raso'va̍ caṣṭe di̱vaḥ śiśu̍ḥ |

ya indu̱rvāra̱māvi̍śat || 9.038.05 ||

e̱ṣa sya pī̱taye̎ su̱to hari̍rarṣati dharṇa̱siḥ |

kranda̱nyoni̍ma̱bhi pri̱yam || 9.038.06 ||

ā̱śura̍rṣa bṛhanmate̱ pari̍ pri̱yeṇa̱ dhāmnā̎ |

yatra̍ de̱vā iti̱ bravan̎ || 9.039.01 ||

pa̱ri̱ṣkṛ̱ṇvannani̍ṣkṛta̱ṃ janā̎ya yā̱taya̱nniṣa̍ḥ |

vṛ̱ṣṭiṃ di̱vaḥ pari̍ srava || 9.039.02 ||

su̱ta e̎ti pa̱vitra̱ ā tviṣi̱ṃ dadhā̎na̱ oja̍sā |

vi̱cakṣā̎ṇo viro̱cayan̎ || 9.039.03 ||

a̱yaṃ sa yo di̱vaspari̍ raghu̱yāmā̎ pa̱vitra̱ ā |

sindho̎rū̱rmā vyakṣa̍rat || 9.039.04 ||

ā̱vivā̎sanparā̱vato̱ atho̎ arvā̱vata̍ su̱taḥ |

indrā̎ya sicyate̱ madhu̍ || 9.039.05 ||

sa̱mī̱cī̱nā a̍nūṣata̱ hari̎ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

yonā̎vṛ̱tasya̍ sīdata || 9.039.06

pu̱nā̱no a̍kramīda̱bhi viśvā̱ mṛdho̱ vica̍rṣaṇiḥ |

śu̱mbhanti̱ vipra̎ṃ dhī̱tibhi̍ḥ || 9.040.01 ||

ā yoni̍maru̱ṇo ru̍ha̱dgama̱dindra̱ṃ vṛṣā̎ su̱taḥ |

dhru̱ve sada̍si sīdati || 9.040.02 ||

nū no̎ ra̱yiṃ ma̱hāmi̎ndo̱'smabhya̎ṃ soma vi̱śvata̍ḥ |

ā pa̍vasva saha̱sriṇam̎ || 9.040.03 ||

viśvā̎ soma pavamāna dyu̱mnānī̎nda̱vā bha̍ra |

vi̱dāḥ sa̍ha̱sriṇī̱riṣa̍ḥ || 9.040.04 ||

sa na̍ punā̱na ā bha̍ra ra̱yiṃ sto̱tre su̱vīryam̎ |

ja̱ri̱turva̍rdhayā̱ gira̍ḥ || 9.040.05 ||

pu̱nā̱na i̎nda̱vā bha̍ra̱ soma̍ dvi̱barha̍saṃ ra̱yim |

vṛṣa̎nnindo na u̱kthyam̎ || 9.040.06 ||

pra ye gāvo̱ na bhūrṇa̍yastve̱ṣā a̱yāso̱ akra̍muḥ |

ghnanta̍ kṛ̱ṣṇāmapa̱ tvacam̎ || 9.041.01 ||

su̱vi̱tasya̍ manāma̱he'ti̱ setu̎ṃ durā̱vyam̎ |

sā̱hvāṃso̱ dasyu̍mavra̱tam || 9.041.02 ||

śṛ̱ṇve vṛ̱ṣṭeri̍va sva̱naḥ pava̍mānasya śu̱ṣmiṇa̍ḥ |

cara̎nti vi̱dyuto̎ di̱vi || 9.041.03 ||

ā pa̍vasva ma̱hīmiṣa̱ṃ goma̍dindo̱ hira̎ṇyavat |

aśvā̎va̱dvāja̍vatsu̱taḥ || 9.041.04 ||

sa pa̍vasva vicarṣaṇa̱ ā ma̱hī roda̍sī pṛṇa |

u̱ṣāḥ sūryo̱ na ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ || 9.041.05 ||

pari̍ ṇaḥ śarma̱yantyā̱ dhāra̍yā soma vi̱śvata̍ḥ |

sarā̎ ra̱seva̍ vi̱ṣṭapam̎ || 9.041.06 ||

ja̱naya̎nroca̱nā di̱vo ja̱naya̎nna̱psu sūryam̎ |

vasā̎no̱ gā a̱po hari̍ḥ || 9.042.01 ||

e̱ṣa pra̱tnena̱ manma̍nā de̱vo de̱vebhya̱spari̍ |

dhāra̍yā pavate su̱taḥ || 9.042.02 ||

vā̱vṛ̱dhā̱nāya̱ tūrva̍ye̱ pava̎nte̱ vāja̍sātaye |

somā̎ sa̱hasra̍pājasaḥ || 9.042.03 ||

du̱hā̱naḥ pra̱tnamitpaya̍ḥ pa̱vitre̱ pari̍ ṣicyate |

kranda̎nde̱vāṃ a̍jījanat || 9.042.04 ||

a̱bhi viśvā̎ni̱ vāryā̱bhi de̱vāṃ ṛ̍tā̱vṛdha̍ḥ |

soma̍ punā̱no a̍rṣati || 9.042.05 ||

goma̎nnaḥ soma vī̱rava̱daśvā̎va̱dvāja̍vatsu̱taḥ |

pava̍sva bṛha̱tīriṣa̍ḥ || 9.042.06 ||

yo atya̍ iva mṛ̱jyate̱ gobhi̱rmadā̎ya harya̱taḥ |

taṃ gī̱rbhirvā̎sayāmasi || 9.043.01 ||

taṃ no̱ viśvā̎ ava̱syuvo̱ gira̍ śumbhanti pū̱rvathā̎ |

indu̱mindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.043.02 ||

pu̱nā̱no yā̎ti harya̱taḥ somo̎ gī̱rbhiḥ pari̍ṣkṛtaḥ |

vipra̍sya̱ medhyā̎titheḥ || 9.043.03 ||

pava̍māna vi̱dā ra̱yima̱smabhya̎ṃ soma su̱śriyam̎ |

indo̎ sa̱hasra̍varcasam || 9.043.04 ||

indu̱ratyo̱ na vā̎ja̱sṛtkani̍kranti pa̱vitra̱ ā |

yadakṣā̱rati̍ deva̱yuḥ || 9.043.05||

pava̍sva̱ vāja̍sātaye̱ vipra̍sya gṛṇa̱to vṛ̱dhe |

soma̱ rāsva̍ su̱vīryam̎ || 9.043.06||

Tritiya Adhyaya

pra ṇa̍ indo ma̱he tana̍ ū̱rmiṃ na bibhra̍darṣasi |

a̱bhi de̱vāṃ a̱yāsya̍ḥ || 9.044.01 ||

ma̱tī ju̱ṣṭo dhi̱yā hi̱taḥ somo̎ hinve parā̱vati̍ |

vipra̍sya̱ dhāra̍yā ka̱viḥ || 9.044.02 ||

a̱yaṃ de̱veṣu̱ jāgṛ̍viḥ su̱ta e̎ti pa̱vitra̱ ā |

somo̎ yāti̱ vica̍rṣaṇiḥ || 9.044.03 ||

sa na̍ pavasva vāja̱yuśca̍krā̱ṇaścāru̍madhva̱ram |

ba̱rhiṣmā̱ṃ ā vi̍vāsati || 9.044.04 ||

sa no̱ bhagā̎ya vā̱yave̱ vipra̍vīraḥ sa̱dāvṛ̍dhaḥ |

somo̎ de̱veṣvā ya̍mat || 9.044.05 ||

sa no̎ a̱dya vasu̍ttaye kratu̱vidgā̎tu̱vitta̍maḥ |

vāja̎ṃ jeṣi̱ śravo̎ bṛ̱hat || 9.044.06 ||

sa pa̍vasva̱ madā̎ya̱ kaṃ nṛ̱cakṣā̎ de̱vavī̎taye |

inda̱vindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.045.01 ||

sa no̎ arṣā̱bhi dū̱tya1̱̍ṃ tvamindrā̎ya tośase |

de̱vānsakhi̍bhya̱ ā varam̎ || 9.045.02 ||

u̱ta tvāma̍ru̱ṇaṃ va̱yaṃ gobhi̍rañjmo̱ madā̎ya̱ kam |

vi no̎ rā̱ye duro̎ vṛdhi || 9.045.03 ||

atyū̎ pa̱vitra̍makramīdvā̱jī dhura̱ṃ na yāma̍ni |

indu̍rde̱veṣu̍ patyate || 9.045.04 ||

samī̱ sakhā̎yo asvara̱nvane̱ krīḷa̎nta̱matya̍vim |

indu̎ṃ nā̱vā a̍nūṣata || 9.045.05 ||

tayā̎ pavasva̱ dhāra̍yā̱ yayā̎ pī̱to vi̱cakṣa̍se |

indo̎ sto̱tre su̱vīryam̎ || 9.045.06 ||

asṛ̍grande̱vavī̎ta̱ye'tyā̎sa̱ kṛtvyā̎ iva |

kṣara̎ntaḥ parvatā̱vṛdha̍ḥ || 9.046.01 ||

pari̍ṣkṛtāsa̱ inda̍vo̱ yoṣe̎va̱ pitryā̎vatī |

vā̱yuṃ somā̎ asṛkṣata || 9.046.02 ||

e̱te somā̎sa̱ inda̍va̱ praya̍svantaśca̱mū su̱tāḥ |

indra̎ṃ vardhanti̱ karma̍bhiḥ || 9.046.03 ||

ā dhā̎vatā suhastyaḥ śu̱krā gṛ̍bhṇīta ma̱nthinā̎ |

gobhi̍ śrīṇīta matsa̱ram || 9.046.04 ||

sa pa̍vasva dhanañjaya praya̱ntā rādha̍so ma̱haḥ |

a̱smabhya̎ṃ soma gātu̱vit || 9.046.05 ||

e̱taṃ mṛ̍janti̱ marjya̱ṃ pava̍māna̱ṃ daśa̱ kṣipa̍ḥ |

indrā̎ya matsa̱raṃ madam̎ || 9.046.06

a̱yā soma̍ sukṛ̱tyayā̎ ma̱haści̍da̱bhya̍vardhata |

ma̱ndā̱na udvṛ̍ṣāyate || 9.047.01 ||

kṛ̱tānīda̍sya̱ kartvā̱ ceta̎nte dasyu̱tarha̍ṇā |

ṛ̱ṇā ca̍ dhṛ̱ṣṇuśca̍yate || 9.047.02 ||

ātsoma̍ indri̱yo raso̱ vajra̍ sahasra̱sā bhu̍vat |

u̱kthaṃ yada̍sya̱ jāya̍te || 9.047.03 ||

sva̱yaṃ ka̱virvi̍dha̱rtari̱ viprā̎ya̱ ratna̍micchati |

yadī̎ marmṛ̱jyate̱ dhiya̍ḥ || 9.047.04 ||

si̱ṣā̱satū̎ rayī̱ṇāṃ vāje̱ṣvarva̍tāmiva |

bhare̎ṣu ji̱gyuṣā̎masi || 9.047.05 ||

taṃ tvā̎ nṛ̱mṇāni̱ bibhra̍taṃ sa̱dhasthe̎ṣu ma̱ho di̱vaḥ |

cāru̎ṃ sukṛ̱tyaye̎mahe || 9.048.01 ||

saṃvṛ̍ktadhṛṣṇumu̱kthya̎ṃ ma̱hāma̍hivrata̱ṃ madam̎ |

śa̱taṃ puro̎ ruru̱kṣaṇa̎m || 9.048.02 ||

ata̍stvā ra̱yima̱bhi rājā̎naṃ sukrato di̱vaḥ |

su̱pa̱rṇo a̍vya̱thirbha̍rat || 9.048.03 ||

viśva̍smā̱ itsva̍rdṛ̱śe sādhā̎raṇaṃ raja̱sturam̎ |

go̱pāmṛ̱tasya̱ virbha̍rat || 9.048.04 ||

adhā̎ hinvā̱na i̎ndri̱yaṃ jyāyo̎ mahi̱tvamā̎naśe |

a̱bhi̱ṣṭi̱kṛdvica̍rṣaṇiḥ || 9.048.05 ||

pava̍sva vṛ̱ṣṭimā su no̱'pāmū̱rmiṃ di̱vaspari̍ |

a̱ya̱kṣmā bṛ̍ha̱tīriṣa̍ḥ || 9.049.01 ||

tayā̎ pavasva̱ dhāra̍yā̱ yayā̱ gāva̍ i̱hāgaman̎ |

janyā̎sa̱ upa̍ no gṛ̱ham || 9.049.02 ||

ghṛ̱taṃ pa̍vasva̱ dhāra̍yā ya̱jñeṣu̍ deva̱vīta̍maḥ |

a̱smabhya̎ṃ vṛ̱ṣṭimā pa̍va || 9.049.03 ||

sa na̍ ū̱rje vya1̱̍ vyaya̎ṃ pa̱vitra̎ṃ dhāva̱ dhāra̍yā |

de̱vāsa̍ śṛ̱ṇava̱nhi kam̎ || 9.049.04 ||

pava̍māno asiṣyada̱drakṣā̎ṃsyapa̱jaṅgha̍nat |

pra̱tna̱vadro̱caya̱nruca̍ḥ || 9.049.05 ||

utte̱ śuṣmā̎sa īrate̱ sindho̎rū̱rmeri̍va sva̱naḥ |

vā̱ṇasya̍ codayā pa̱vim || 9.050.01 ||

pra̱sa̱ve ta̱ udī̎rate ti̱sro vāco̎ makha̱syuva̍ḥ |

yadavya̱ eṣi̱ sāna̍vi || 9.050.02 ||

avyo̱ vāre̱ pari̍ pri̱yaṃ hari̍ṃ hinva̱ntyadri̍bhiḥ |

pava̍mānaṃ madhu̱ścutam̎ || 9.050.03 ||

ā pa̍vasva madintama pa̱vitra̱ṃ dhāra̍yā kave |

a̱rkasya̱ yoni̍mā̱sada̍m || 9.050.04 ||

sa pa̍vasva madintama̱ gobhi̍rañjā̱no a̱ktubhi̍ḥ |

inda̱vindrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ || 9.050.05 ||

adhva̍ryo̱ adri̍bhiḥ su̱taṃ soma̎ṃ pa̱vitra̱ ā sṛ̍ja |

pu̱nī̱hīndrā̎ya̱ pāta̍ve || 9.051.01 ||

di̱vaḥ pī̱yūṣa̍mutta̱maṃ soma̱mindrā̎ya va̱jriṇe̎ |

su̱notā̱ madhu̍mattamam || 9.051.02 ||

tava̱ tya i̎ndo̱ andha̍so de̱vā madho̱rvya̍śnate |

pava̍mānasya ma̱ruta̍ḥ || 9.051.03 ||

tvaṃ hi so̎ma va̱rdhaya̎nsu̱to madā̎ya̱ bhūrṇa̍ye |

vṛṣa̎nsto̱tāra̍mū̱taye̎ || 9.051.04 ||

a̱bhya̍rṣa vicakṣaṇa pa̱vitra̱ṃ dhāra̍yā su̱taḥ |

a̱bhi vāja̍mu̱ta śrava̍ḥ || 9.051.05 ||

pari̍ dyu̱kṣaḥ sa̱nadra̍yi̱rbhara̱dvāja̎ṃ no̱ andha̍sā |

su̱vā̱no a̍rṣa pa̱vitra̱ ā || 9.052.01 ||

tava̍ pra̱tnebhi̱radhva̍bhi̱ravyo̱ vāre̱ pari̍ pri̱yaḥ |

sa̱hasra̍dhāro yā̱ttanā̎ || 9.052.02 ||

ca̱rurna yastamī̎ṅkha̱yendo̱ na dāna̍mīṅkhaya |

va̱dhairva̍dhasnavīṅkhaya || 9.052.03 ||

ni śuṣma̍mindaveṣā̱ṃ puru̍hūta̱ janā̎nām |

yo a̱smāṃ ā̱dide̎śati || 9.052.04 ||

śa̱taṃ na̍ inda ū̱tibhi̍ sa̱hasra̎ṃ vā̱ śucī̎nām |

pava̍sva maṃha̱yadra̍yiḥ || 9.052.05 ||

utte̱ śuṣmā̎so asthū̱ rakṣo̎ bhi̱ndanto̎ adrivaḥ |

nu̱dasva̱ yāḥ pa̍ri̱spṛdha̍ḥ || 9.053.01 ||

a̱yā ni̍ja̱ghniroja̍sā rathasa̱ṅge dhane̎ hi̱te |

stavā̱ abi̍bhyuṣā hṛ̱dā || 9.053.02 ||

asya̍ vra̱tāni̱ nādhṛṣe̱ pava̍mānasya dū̱ḍhyā̎ |

ru̱ja yastvā̎ pṛta̱nyati̍ || 9.053.03 ||

taṃ hi̎nvanti mada̱cyuta̱ṃ hari̎ṃ na̱dīṣu̍ vā̱jinam̎ |

indu̱mindrā̎ya matsa̱ram || 9.053.04 ||

a̱sya pra̱tnāmanu̱ dyuta̎ṃ śu̱kraṃ du̍duhre̱ ahra̍yaḥ |

paya̍ sahasra̱sāmṛṣim̎ || 9.054.01 ||

a̱yaṃ sūrya̍ ivopa̱dṛga̱yaṃ sarā̎ṃsi dhāvati |

sa̱pta pra̱vata̱ ā divam̎ || 9.054.02 ||

a̱yaṃ viśvā̎ni tiṣṭhati punā̱no bhuva̍no̱pari̍ |

somo̎ de̱vo na sūrya̍ḥ || 9.054.03 ||

pari̍ ṇo de̱vavī̎taye̱ vājā̎ṃ arṣasi̱ goma̍taḥ |

pu̱nā̱na i̎ndavindra̱yuḥ || 9.054.04 ||

yava̎ṃyavaṃ no̱ andha̍sā pu̱ṣṭampu̍ṣṭa̱ṃ pari̍ srava |

soma̱ viśvā̎ ca̱ saubha̍gā || 9.055.01 ||

indo̱ yathā̱ tava̱ stavo̱ yathā̎ te jā̱tamandha̍saḥ |

ni ba̱rhiṣi̍ pri̱ye sa̍daḥ || 9.055.02 ||

u̱ta no̎ go̱vida̍śva̱vitpava̍sva so̱māndha̍sā |

ma̱kṣūta̍mebhi̱raha̍bhiḥ || 9.055.03 ||

yo ji̱nāti̱ na jīya̍te̱ hanti̱ śatru̍ma̱bhītya̍ |

sa pa̍vasva sahasrajit || 9.055.04 ||

pari̱ soma̍ ṛ̱taṃ bṛ̱hadā̱śuḥ pa̱vitre̎ arṣati |

vi̱ghnanrakṣā̎ṃsi deva̱yuḥ || 9.056.01 ||

yatsomo̱ vāja̱marṣa̍ti śa̱taṃ dhārā̎ apa̱syuva̍ḥ |

indra̍sya sa̱khyamā̎vi̱śan || 9.056.02 ||

a̱bhi tvā̱ yoṣa̍ṇo̱ daśa̍ jā̱raṃ na ka̱nyā̎nūṣata |

mṛ̱jyase̎ soma sā̱taye̎ || 9.056.03 ||

tvamindrā̎ya̱ viṣṇa̍ve svā̱duri̎ndo̱ pari̍ srava |

nṛn sto̱tṛn pā̱pyaṃha̍saḥ || 9.056.04 ||

pra te̱ dhārā̎ asa̱ścato̎ di̱vo na ya̍nti vṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ |

acchā̱ vāja̎ṃ saha̱sriṇam̎ || 9.057.01 ||

a̱bhi pri̱yāṇi̱ kāvyā̱ viśvā̱ cakṣā̎ṇo arṣati |

hari̍stuñjā̱na āyu̍dhā || 9.057.02 ||

sa ma̎rmṛjā̱na ā̱yubhi̱ribho̱ rāje̎va suvra̱taḥ |

śye̱no na vaṃsu̍ ṣīdati || 9.057.03 ||

sa no̱ viśvā̎ di̱vo vasū̱to pṛ̍thi̱vyā adhi̍ |

pu̱nā̱na i̎nda̱vā bha̍ra || 9.057.04 ||

tara̱tsa ma̱ndī dhā̎vati̱ dhārā̎ su̱tasyāndha̍saḥ |

tara̱tsa ma̱ndī dhā̎vati || 9.058.01 ||

u̱srā ve̎da̱ vasū̎nā̱ṃ marta̍sya de̱vyava̍saḥ |

tara̱tsa ma̱ndī dhā̎vati || 9.058.02 ||

dhva̱srayo̎ puru̱ṣantyo̱rā sa̱hasrā̎ṇi dadmahe |

tara̱tsa ma̱ndī dhā̎vati || 9.058.03 ||

ā yayo̎stri̱ṃśata̱ṃ tanā̎ sa̱hasrā̎ṇi ca̱ dadma̍he |

tara̱tsa ma̱ndī dhā̎vati || 9.058.04 ||

pava̍sva go̱jida̍śva̱jidvi̍śva̱jitso̎ma raṇya̱jit |

pra̱jāva̱dratna̱mā bha̍ra || 9.059.01 ||

pava̍svā̱d‌bhyo adā̎bhya̱ pava̱svauṣa̍dhībhyaḥ |

pava̍sva dhi̱ṣaṇā̎bhyaḥ || 9.059.02 ||

tvaṃ so̎ma̱ pava̍māno̱ viśvā̎ni duri̱tā ta̍ra |

ka̱viḥ sī̎da̱ ni ba̱rhiṣi̍ || 9.059.03 ||

pava̍māna̱ sva̍rvido̱ jāya̍māno'bhavo ma̱hān |

indo̱ viśvā̎ṃ a̱bhīda̍si || 9.059.04 ||

pra gā̎ya̱treṇa̍ gāyata̱ pava̍māna̱ṃ vica̍rṣaṇim |

indu̎ṃ sa̱hasra̍cakṣasam || 9.060.01 ||

taṃ tvā̎ sa̱hasra̍cakṣasa̱matho̎ sa̱hasra̍bharṇasam |

ati̱ vāra̍mapāviṣuḥ || 9.060.02 ||

ati̱ vārā̱npava̍māno asiṣyadatka̱laśā̎ṃ a̱bhi dhā̎vati |

indra̍sya̱ hārdyā̎vi̱śan || 9.060.03 ||

indra̍sya soma̱ rādha̍se̱ śaṃ pa̍vasva vicarṣaṇe |

pra̱jāva̱dreta̱ ā bha̍ra || 9.060.04 ||

a̱yā vī̱tī pari̍ srava̱ yasta̍ indo̱ made̱ṣvā |

a̱vāha̎nnava̱tīrnava̍ || 9.061.01 ||

pura̍ sa̱dya i̱tthādhi̍ye̱ divo̎dāsāya̱ śamba̍ram |

adha̱ tyaṃ tu̱rvaśa̱ṃ yadum̎ || 9.061.02 ||

pari̍ ṇo̱ aśva̍maśva̱vidgoma̍dindo̱ hira̎ṇyavat |

kṣarā̎ saha̱sriṇī̱riṣa̍ḥ || 9.061.03 ||

pava̍mānasya te va̱yaṃ pa̱vitra̍mabhyunda̱taḥ |

sa̱khi̱tvamā vṛ̍ṇīmahe || 9.061.04 ||

ye te̎ pa̱vitra̍mū̱rmayo̎'bhi̱kṣara̎nti̱ dhāra̍yā |

tebhi̎rnaḥ soma mṛḷaya || 9.061.05 ||

sa na̍ punā̱na ā bha̍ra ra̱yiṃ vī̱rava̍tī̱miṣam̎ |

īśā̎naḥ soma vi̱śvata̍ḥ || 9.061.06 ||

e̱tamu̱ tyaṃ daśa̱ kṣipo̎ mṛ̱janti̱ sindhu̍mātaram |

samā̎di̱tyebhi̍rakhyata || 9.061.07 ||

samindre̎ṇo̱ta vā̱yunā̎ su̱ta e̎ti pa̱vitra̱ ā |

saṃ sūrya̍sya ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ || 9.061.08 ||

sa no̱ bhagā̎ya vā̱yave̎ pū̱ṣṇe pa̍vasva̱ madhu̍mān |

cāru̍rmi̱tre varu̍ṇe ca || 9.061.09 ||

u̱ccā te̎ jā̱tamandha̍so di̱vi ṣadbhūmyā da̍de |

u̱graṃ śarma̱ mahi̱ śrava̍ḥ || 9.061.10 ||

e̱nā viśvā̎nya̱rya ā dyu̱mnāni̱ mānu̍ṣāṇām |

siṣā̎santo vanāmahe || 9.061.11 ||

sa na̱ indrā̎ya̱ yajya̍ve̱ varu̍ṇāya ma̱rud‌bhya̍ḥ |

va̱ri̱vo̱vitpari̍ srava || 9.061.12 ||

upo̱ ṣu jā̱tama̱ptura̱ṃ gobhi̍rbha̱ṅgaṃ pari̍ṣkṛtam |

indu̎ṃ de̱vā a̍yāsiṣuḥ || 9.061.13 ||

tamidva̍rdhantu no̱ giro̎ va̱tsaṃ sa̱ṃśiśva̍rīriva |

ya indra̍sya hṛda̱ṃsani̍ḥ || 9.061.14 ||

arṣā̎ ṇaḥ soma̱ śaṃ gave̎ dhu̱kṣasva̍ pi̱pyuṣī̱miṣam̎ |

vardhā̎ samu̱dramu̱kthyam̎ || 9.061.15 ||

pava̍māno ajījanaddi̱vaści̱traṃ na ta̎nya̱tum |

jyoti̍rvaiśvāna̱raṃ bṛ̱hat || 9.061.16 ||

pava̍mānasya te̱ raso̱ mado̎ rājannaducchu̱naḥ |

vi vāra̱mavya̍marṣati || 9.061.17 ||

pava̍māna̱ rasa̱stava̱ dakṣo̱ vi rā̎jati dyu̱mān |

jyoti̱rviśva̱ṃ sva̍rdṛ̱śe || 9.061.18 ||

yaste̱ mado̱ vare̎ṇya̱stenā̎ pava̱svāndha̍sā |

de̱vā̱vīra̍ghaśaṃsa̱hā || 9.061.19 ||

jaghni̍rvṛ̱trama̍mi̱triya̱ṃ sasni̱rvāja̎ṃ di̱vedi̍ve |

go̱ṣā u̍ aśva̱sā a̍si || 9.061.20 ||

sammi̍ślo aru̱ṣo bha̍va sūpa̱sthābhi̱rna dhe̱nubhi̍ḥ |

sīda̎ñchye̱no na yoni̱mā || 9.061.21 ||

sa pa̍vasva̱ ya āvi̱thendra̎ṃ vṛ̱trāya̱ hanta̍ve |

va̱vri̱vāṃsa̎ṃ ma̱hīra̱paḥ || 9.061.22 ||

su̱vīrā̎so va̱yaṃ dhanā̱ jaye̎ma soma mīḍh‌vaḥ |

pu̱nā̱no va̍rdha no̱ gira̍ḥ || 9.061.23 ||

tvotā̎sa̱stavāva̍sā̱ syāma̍ va̱nvanta̍ ā̱mura̍ḥ |

soma̍ vra̱teṣu̍ jāgṛhi || 9.061.24 ||

a̱pa̱ghnanpa̍vate̱ mṛdho'pa̱ somo̱ arā̎vṇaḥ |

gaccha̱nnindra̍sya niṣkṛ̱tam || 9.061.25 ||

ma̱ho no̎ rā̱ya ā bha̍ra̱ pava̍māna ja̱hī mṛdha̍ḥ |

rāsve̎ndo vī̱rava̱dyaśa̍ḥ || 9.061.26 ||

na tvā̎ śa̱taṃ ca̱na hruto̱ rādho̱ ditsa̎nta̱mā mi̍nan |

yatpu̍nā̱no ma̍kha̱syase̎ || 9.061.27 ||

pava̍svendo̱ vṛṣā̎ su̱taḥ kṛ̱dhī no̎ ya̱śaso̱ jane̎ |

viśvā̱ apa̱ dviṣo̎ jahi || 9.061.28 ||

asya̍ te sa̱khye va̱yaṃ tave̎ndo dyu̱mna u̍tta̱me |

sā̱sa̱hyāma̍ pṛtanya̱taḥ || 9.061.29 ||

yā te̎ bhī̱mānyāyu̍dhā ti̱gmāni̱ santi̱ dhūrva̍ṇe |

rakṣā̎ samasya no ni̱daḥ || 9.061.30 ||

e̱te a̍sṛgra̱minda̍vasti̱raḥ pa̱vitra̍mā̱śava̍ḥ |

viśvā̎nya̱bhi saubha̍gā || 9.062.01 ||

vi̱ghnanto̎ duri̱tā pu̱ru su̱gā to̱kāya̍ vā̱jina̍ḥ |

tanā̎ kṛ̱ṇvanto̱ arva̍te || 9.062.02 ||

kṛ̱ṇvanto̱ vari̍vo̱ gave̱'bhya̍rṣanti suṣṭu̱tim |

iḷā̎ma̱smabhya̎ṃ sa̱ṃyatam̎ || 9.062.03 ||

asā̎vya̱ṃśurmadā̎yā̱psu dakṣo̎ giri̱ṣṭhāḥ |

śye̱no na yoni̱māsa̍dat || 9.062.04 ||

śu̱bhramandho̎ de̱vavā̎tama̱psu dhū̱to nṛbhi̍ su̱taḥ |

svada̎nti̱ gāva̱ payo̎bhiḥ || 9.062.05 ||

ādī̱maśva̱ṃ na hetā̱ro'śū̎śubhanna̱mṛtā̎ya |

madhvo̱ rasa̎ṃ sadha̱māde̎ || 9.062.06 ||

yāste̱ dhārā̎ madhu̱ścuto'sṛ̍graminda ū̱taye̎ |

tābhi̍ pa̱vitra̱māsa̍daḥ || 9.062.07 ||

so a̱rṣendrā̎ya pī̱taye̎ ti̱ro romā̎ṇya̱vyayā̎ |

sīda̱nyonā̱ vane̱ṣvā || 9.062.08 ||

tvami̎ndo̱ pari̍ srava̱ svādi̍ṣṭho̱ aṅgi̍robhyaḥ |

va̱ri̱vo̱vidghṛ̱taṃ paya̍ḥ || 9.062.09 ||

a̱yaṃ vica̍rṣaṇirhi̱taḥ pava̍māna̱ sa ce̎tati |

hi̱nvā̱na āpya̎ṃ bṛ̱hat || 9.062.10 ||

e̱ṣa vṛṣā̱ vṛṣa̍vrata̱ pava̍māno aśasti̱hā |

kara̱dvasū̎ni dā̱śuṣe̎ || 9.062.11 ||

ā pa̍vasva saha̱sriṇa̎ṃ ra̱yiṃ goma̎ntama̱śvinam̎ |

pu̱ru̱śca̱ndraṃ pu̍ru̱spṛham̎ || 9.062.12 ||

e̱ṣa sya pari̍ ṣicyate marmṛ̱jyamā̎na ā̱yubhi̍ḥ |

u̱ru̱gā̱yaḥ ka̱vikra̍tuḥ || 9.062.13 ||

sa̱hasro̎tiḥ śa̱tāma̍gho vi̱māno̱ raja̍saḥ ka̱viḥ |

indrā̎ya pavate̱ mada̍ḥ || 9.062.14

gi̱rā jā̱ta i̱ha stu̱ta indu̱rindrā̎ya dhīyate |

viryonā̎ vasa̱tāvi̍va || 9.062.15

pava̍mānaḥ su̱to nṛbhi̱ somo̱ vāja̍mivāsarat |

ca̱mūṣu̱ śakma̍nā̱sadam̎ || 9.062.16

taṃ tri̍pṛ̱ṣṭhe tri̍vandhu̱re rathe̎ yuñjanti̱ yāta̍ve |

ṛṣī̎ṇāṃ sa̱pta dhī̱tibhi̍ḥ || 9.062.17

taṃ so̎tāro dhana̱spṛta̍mā̱śuṃ vājā̎ya̱ yāta̍ve |

hari̎ṃ hinota vā̱jinam̎ || 9.062.18

ā̱vi̱śanka̱laśa̎ṃ su̱to viśvā̱ arṣa̎nna̱bhi śriya̍ḥ |

śūro̱ na goṣu̍ tiṣṭhati || 9.062.19

ā ta̍ indo̱ madā̎ya̱ kaṃ payo̎ duhantyā̱yava̍ḥ |
